# GM ONLY - Ledger, cumulative S1-S7 (spoilers). Built 2026-10-07 from: S06 cumulative ledger (S1-S6 Part 1) + S06 Part 2 / S07 working ledger. Nothing edited; joined in order.

# GM LEDGER - PRIVATE - L1 Solo Playtest (Caria) - started 2026-09-27

## Timing vs Saltwind events (@@* GM decision)
- Game opens ~2 weeks after Saltwind S02. Late autumn.
- The zealot priest is back at the monastery, humiliated, still hunting his ghola. The Saltwind story reaches the monastery as garbled gossip: a village shrine-keeper "witch" and a mob of fishwives drove off a priest of Rikitak. Caria may hear it in the kitchens.
- Monastery spies are riding with the next caravan to Saltwind (Karinna expects this).

## NPCs
### Asher (ember sprite, Caria's companion)
- Level 1; Manifestation 1; Hook: Smitten with the Child of the Contract 1
- His Hook's phrasing implies he perceives something about Caria's ancestry ("Child of the Contract"). Lee flagged it with a wink. WHAT ASHER KNOWS: @@ decide - hold back; reveal only through play.

## The Monastery (Lee, 2026-09-27 - canon for this game)
- Fewer than threescore ordained, including the offices (Western-monastic names used here; in-house titles differ - @@ TBD): Prior, Cellarer, Infirmarer, Almoner, Precentor, Sacrist, Hospitaller. Fewer laypeople: friars, craftsmen, common laborers.
- Gardens: very functional, mostly medicinal.
- Recent politics: richer and LESS popular.
- ALMONER: role reversed - now effectively Inquisitor-Collector. Has absorbed much of the Cellarer's work.
- CELLARER (also Sacrist): pushed aside by the Almoner; in turn took over most of the Hospitaller's duties.
- HOSPITALLER: dead late last year - "misidentified mushrooms," very unpleasantly.
- PRECENTOR = the old priest who collected Caria. Still formidable; age creeping. Increasingly at odds with the Almoner and the Prior - "on the outs." Still in charge of teaching. Loyal people scattered through the house and the countryside.
- Precentor arranged, many seasons ago, for an Atlantean Sorcerer to train the first Spellweavers at the Temple in Ganth. Never caught on. He later brought one of those early students to teach Caria first principles.

## Key NPCs (@@* all GM-invented unless noted)
### Master Ivro Tamsett (@@* name) - PRECENTOR; the old priest who brought her (Lee: old, watchful, protective; no one dared abuse her; on the outs with Almoner and Prior; runs teaching; loyal network)
- Healer of the old faith; loyal to Rikitak as he was. Knows the house is sick; does NOT know it is Tahanos. Suspects the purifier faction.
- Recognized her mark when she was 9. Took her as payment on purpose - to get her out of the village before someone worse noticed. Has kept her conspicuously *valuable* (star pupil) on the theory that a prized asset is harder to make disappear.
- Aging, and quietly failing (a chest complaint he treats himself and hides). His protection has a clock.
- Why "no one dared": partly Ivro's standing, partly that a few who tried to get close found themselves unaccountably unwell (Asher? her aura? @@ undecided - leave ambiguous).
### The zealot priest (canon, unnamed) - @@* name: Hierarch Voss Calder; coachman Olwin (canon)
- Leader of the "sin and purification" faction. Tahanos's man, knowingly or not (@@ knowingly).
- Wants his ghola back. Humiliated at Saltwind; needs a win.
- Has a list of the "sin-marked" in the house. Caria is on it. She doesn't know.
### Asher
- see above.

## What her mark actually is (@@* SECRET - reveal only through play)
- Her great-grandmother struck a bargain with a demon of Longing - an emotion-spirit of the "true, pure love" kind, reckoned one of Rikitak's own - and the hedge-witch grandmother was the child of that contract. The contract was never closed: its terms still bind the bloodline, and it shows in the aura. Asher sees the open contract - hence "Child of the Contract."
- Consequence: the faithful could read her as marked by Rikitak's own; the purifiers read her as sin incarnate; Tahanos's people would read her as an unclosed contract they might *acquire*.
### Names she has overheard for it (player-facing - give to Lee before play)
- "ember-blooded" (kitchen talk, not unkind), "cambion-touched" (scholars, arguing), "Longing's get" (one old master, once, sharply hushed), "sin-marked" (the purifiers).

## Pressures / clocks
- PURIFICATION LIST [0/4]: Calder's faction moves against the sin-marked. At 4, Caria is summoned for "examination."
- IVRO'S HEALTH [1/4]: at 4 he is bedridden/dying; protection lapses.
- GHOLA: loose. Last near the Gallows Teeth. Calder's agents search. (Do NOT tie to the Warden or to Min's Hobbe without asking Lee.)
- ASHER'S DEBT DANCE: currently Caria is owed (slightly). Track.

## GM Luck Tokens
- Start: max(1 player, Level 2) + 1 = 3; +1 per scene if room (@@ GM cap assumed = starting count, 3)

## Monastery ranks (GM-defined @@* - Lee delegated)
- Acolyte -> Novice -> Initiate -> (higher ranks TBD). Advancement by demonstrated accomplishment (roughly total Karma), granted by masters.
- Initiate privileges (player-facing @@*): may bind in the lesser ritual chamber without a master present, but each binding is entered in the chamber book (so her current bindings are a matter of record); may run errands outside the walls on a master's writ; may be assigned juniors to supervise.

## GM-private notes on Lee's monastery canon (@@*)
- The Hospitaller's "mushrooms": murder. The Hospitaller kept the guest-house open to all comers and objected to the Almoner's seizures; he was in the way. Nobody has proven anything. (Note the danger to Caria: a quiet student with a *poison* spell in her repertoire, living by the kitchens, is a convenient suspect if anyone ever needs one.)
- Caria's first-principles tutor: @@ name/whereabouts TBD - one of the Ganth Temple's early Spellweavers.
- OPEN with Lee: is the Saltwind zealot priest the Almoner himself, or the Almoner's hound?

## SESSION 1 - Scene 1: The Library (midmorning) - started 2026-09-27
- Caria Tokens 2/2 (no refill - at cap). GM Tokens 3/3.
- Tobin Marle (@@*): Precentor's clerk, Novice, mid-twenties, fussy, guards Ivro's door and appointment book. SECRET: paid by the Almoner's office to note who visits the Precentor. Resents Caria's easy access over the years.
- Behind the study door RIGHT NOW: Hierarch Voss Calder (the Saltwind priest - light contralto voice, tattooed scalp) is pressing Ivro to hand over the lesser ritual chamber's book and the roll of students admitted to it, "for the Almoner's accounting." Ivro is refusing. Ivro coughs - worse than he lets on.
- If Caria is seen by Calder here: PURIFICATION LIST may tick.
- Caria took 1 Karma for attendance (unspent; usable as emergency/upgraded Token until end of session).
- NPCs named by Lee: Brother Gavin (old lay brother, doddering, perhaps a zealot, kind and sweet; drools on books); Brother Basil (pompous old blowhard, means well). Both dozing in the library.
- S1 beat: Calder exits the study and finds Caria at Tobin's desk. Hook FIRED as escalation, NOT paid: Lee ruled no Karma - unpleasant but doesn't derail her plans. Caria Karma stays 1 unspent.
- PURIFICATION LIST -> 1/4 (Calder now links Caria to Ivro's defiance over the chamber book).
- Caria publicly needled Calder with "a nice mushroom tea" (the Hospitaller's death) in front of Tobin. PURIFICATION LIST -> 2/4. Calder will remember; Tobin will report it.
- Scene 2: Precentor's study. Calder gone. Caria stands to attention before Ivro. (Tokens unchanged: Caria 2/2, GM 3/3.)
- Ivro knows: Calder demanded the lesser chamber book + roll of admitted students "for the Almoner's accounting"; Ivro refused, for now. Ivro knows Caria's bindings are in that book. He will NOT tell her what Calder's list is (he only suspects it exists). He's frightened for her and furious at her for the mushroom crack.
- Caria answered Ivro's "Promise me" with "Understood, my lord" - NOT a promise. Ivro noticed.
- Caria asks leave to visit regional shrines: Grolikarnigan's outside Green Haven; Susheil's in Saltwind; Rana's on the bluff above Saltwind. Ivro grants it - it gets her out of Calder's sight while he fights over the chamber book.
- Travel: the next caravan down the North Road passes in 2 days. (Karinna expects monastery spies in that caravan - the Almoner's agents ride with it.)
- Ivro will want her to carry a letter (next beat): to the Saltwind shrine-keeper who faced down Calder - Mira. He's heard the gossip and is delighted; he's reaching for allies.
- Ivro's errand: a sealed letter (his PERSONAL seal - a salamander - not the Precentor's office seal) for the Saltwind shrine-keeper, "Thousand-Voices" (Mira). Into her hand only. Contents (GM only @@*): Ivro introduces himself as a friend of the old faith; asks what Calder was hunting there; offers the shelter of his network; warns that the Almoner's collectors ride the caravans. He asks her to judge Caria for herself.
- Ivro tells Caria (now her knowledge): Calder went to the coast "to recover the Almoner's property" and came back empty-handed and furious; the Almoner's collectors ride the caravans lately - travel as a pilgrim, only a pilgrim.
- Route: caravan passes Crossroads before Saltwind; Caria does the errand first (Lee), Green Haven after.
- Scene 3 (montage, same afternoon): library -> cell. Tokens: Caria 2/2, GM 3/3 (both at cap).
- Library novice Merrit Holloway (@@*), ~21, sweet on Caria after two minutes of attention (she wanted it - color, no Karma). Provides: regional map copy; circuit-priest visitation register (Saltwind: Susheil shrine kept by blind elder Karinna; "an unordained hedge shrine-keeper also serves" - Mira); caravan: master Dasha Korrin of Ganth, 11 wagons, due in 2 days. Scriptorium issued travel papers for two passengers from the Almoner's office: Clerk Fennick, and "a brother" with no name on the slip.
- Tobin: Caria apologised, civil. He accepted stiffly, off-balance. Seed: Tobin is conflicted (he's paid to report her; she was kind). Not yet turned.
- Cell: Third Eye opened. Letter traces read FAILED (tie). But the seal is plainly warded to Second Sight: a tiny bound spark in the salamander wax - a watcher that would know if the seal were broken. Asher sees her open the eye.
- Hook used for this scene (+1 on the reading).
- Merrit Holloway: freckled, gangly, sharp; wants to be a cartographer, copies maps for love. Caria touched his face in thanks -> he is now thoroughly smitten (she wanted the attention: color, no Karma). Will likely resurface: gossip, jealousy, or devotion.
- Library had no Saltwind village plan; a coastal survey chart shows the pier, a cove west of the village, and a hazard marked "Gallows Teeth."
- Dasha Korrin (@@*): Ganth merchant, hard bargainer, reputed fair; resents the monastery's "road taxes" and has been squeezed by collectors this year. Potentially sympathetic.
- Clerk Fennick (@@*): Almoner's junior clerk, thirties, thin, meticulous - since the Almoner absorbed the Cellarer's work, he counts the kitchen stores. Caria knows him as "the man who counts onions." The unnamed brother: unknown to her.
- Asher came unbidden: he felt her distress this morning, and he is curious/jealous of the warding spark in the seal (another fire-thing in HIS cell).
- Seal-spark: acknowledged Caria's courtesy with a slow dim-and-brighten; did not speak. (It will report to Ivro that the seal was *examined* but not broken - only if asked. It is loyal to Ivro.)
- ASHER'S ERRAND: Caria asked him to scout Saltwind - the people's mood, "what could burn and what would not," the strength of the Susheil shrine's Anchor. Price named by Asher: a secret she has never told anyone, told to the flame, on his return. Terms accepted -> on his return she owes one secret; paying it closes the deal.
- Asher travels hearth to hearth along the Borderlands; returns in two nights (just before the caravan).
- CONSEQUENCE HOOK in Saltwind (GM): a demon-coloured fire sprite asking "what burns" in a village that just faced down a Rikitak priest. Min senses spirits without Second Sight; Shimmer is an undine (water - natural antipathy); Mira has Second Sight. Roll Asher's discretion vs their notice when he arrives. Whatever they see, they'll remember when Caria walks in.
- Caria bound herself: if she can't pay a suitable secret, she will honor a renegotiation, paying in SERVICE. Asher accepted with delight (open-ended service owed to a smitten fire sprite - a lever for later). Asher departed at dusk.
- Scene 4: the kitchens, evening. Tokens at cap (Caria 2, GM 3).
- Grain-factor's daughter (@@*): Wenna Tarro, ~12, daughter of Olef Tarro, grain factor of Ganth. Held in the west wing as SURETY - a hostage for her father's grain deliveries at the Almoner's price. Fed, kept, confined, frightened; used as leverage only. (Keep it there: coercion/extortion, nothing darker.) Caria glimpsed her once, escorted by two liveried men.
- The laundresses: at least one (Old Sabeth's girl, Nell @@*) already answered the clerks' question - Caria's robes were named. Mags suspects it. -> supports PURIFICATION LIST 2/4 (already ticked). Do not tick again for this.
- Olwin (canon: Calder's coachman, bloodied cudgel): keeps Calder's carriage and horses in the stables when not driving. Leers at the maids. Drinking since the coast - Calder blamed him for the Saltwind riot. Not travelling with the caravan (for now).

## Day 2 (one day before caravan)
- Caria's robe smells faintly of sulphur after one day's wear, though she hasn't worked the Cloud in a fortnight; fresh-laundered robe smells clean. (@@* GM: the Hook "A Hint of Brimstone" is literal - her body carries the faintest brimstone note. Nobody's nose but a suspicious one notices. Not yet confirmed to her.)
- She dressed to be pretty: cerulean silk blouse, breastplate, sword (parazonium), hairpin, breeks, girdle, skirt (hem-circle), open robe. Oiled with cinnamon and clove (masks sulphur - nice). Left satchel and staff in her cell.
- Refectory at breakfast: Precentor's seat at the high table EMPTY. Almoner (@@* name: Almoner Odo Brask - heavy, soft-spoken, rings on every finger) at the high table with Calder beside him. Prior (@@* Prior Aldric Feyne - gaunt, silent, watches everyone) presiding.
- OPEN: where is Ivro's letter? Ask Lee before assuming. If it's in the cell: Fennick's people search cells of the "sulphur" list this morning during breakfast.
- Letter + writ: ON Caria (purse under her arm beneath the robe, braced). Safe. She plans to ask Merrit for better travel protection (oilcloth/scroll case) today.
- High table headcount: Prior Feyne, Almoner Brask, Hierarch Calder, Cellarer-Sacrist (@@* Brother Tamas Oake - old, stooped, pushed aside, seated at the far end), Infirmarer (@@* Sister Hannet Mawr - brisk, sixties, eating calmly -> Ivro not in crisis today). Hospitaller's seat: removed. Precentor: ABSENT.
- STRANGER at the high table's far end: the unnamed "brother" from the travel slip. (@@* Brother Anselm Kade - the Almoner's "finder": lean, weathered, grey-shot beard, road-tanned; a Conjuror who hunts the dead. He rides the caravan to Saltwind to find Calder's lost ghola and to take the measure of the shrine-keeper.) Caria doesn't know him.
- WHILE AT BREAKFAST: Fennick and a lay servant search the cells of the "sulphur" names. Caria's cell: satchel gone through, components tallied; a twist of sulphur powder "confiscated for inventory." Staff untouched. She'll find it on return. PURIFICATION LIST -> 3/4.
- Scene 5 (refectory): Caria opened Third Eye publicly. EXTREME SUCCESS (3/3 = EL3). No Token (at cap 2/2). Marked: +1 Karma credited toward CONJUROR at session end. Immediate benefit: exceptionally clear sight - full reads this scene as if she had Aura Reading (a Boost of +1 on any aura-reading roll this scene).
- Kade's bound hound-spirit (a lean grey dead-dog tracker, Reactive, leashed to Kade) lifted its head toward her the moment her eye opened - HOOK (aura draws spirits) - and Kade noticed via the hound: 2 vs 1, effect 1 -> Kade now KNOWS the Precentor's girl in the blue silk has Second Sight and opened it at breakfast. He does not know what she saw.
- Hook trigger offered as Karma (derailed a covert look into being marked by a spirit-hunter). Pending Lee's ruling.
- What she sees (GM): Prior - grey, shuttered, controlled, unreadable; Almoner - warm contented glow of appetite, and under/behind it a cold thread like grave-air running down through the floor toward the west wing (corruption; NOT identified); Calder - feverish, over-bright, a fire eating its own wick (mad); Tamas Oake - dim, tired, honest grief; Hannet Mawr - steady green; Kade - aura banked down tight (hardened), with the hound at his feet; Merrit - pink, helpless; the hall itself - the house's spiritual current runs *wrong*, like a draft blowing the wrong way through a flue.
- Lee took the Hook Karma. Caria Karma: 2 unspent (attendance + Hook) + 1 earmarked to Conjuror (Extreme) at session end. Hook's first Karma trigger this session is now used - further triggers this session pay Luck Tokens.
- She closed the Eye and kept her head down.
- End of breakfast: Prior Feyne announces (at the Almoner's counsel) the lesser ritual chamber is CLOSED pending audit; all existing bindings to be re-registered with the Almoner's clerks. This circumvents Ivro's refusal. Caria cannot rebind in the house now; field bindings only (at penalty) once she leaves.
- Merrit on Ivro: "Tobin says he's keeping to his study today. Sister Hannet looked in on him before the dawn office."
- Caria fished Fennick (tie 1-1, defender holds): he gave nothing extra. BUT her knowing he's on the caravan tipped him that the scriptorium talked - he looked straight at Merrit. Seed: Merrit may be questioned/punished for the leak.
- Fennick: the carriage is the Hierarch's, not the Almoner's to lend; Olwin stays. He rides a wagon like anyone. "Everything in this House is the Almoner's business now. That is rather the point of an audit."
- Caria told Merrit "it wasn't you that told me" and implied the Precentor as her source (a cover). If Fennick checks with Ivro, Ivro will cover for her - and be quietly furious. Tobin, at his desk within earshot, heard "haunted." (Tobin's report may include it.)
- Merrit steadied by her touch; resolves to help.
- Caria asked Tobin about Ivro: FAILED (0 vs 2). Tobin gives only the official line: resting on the Infirmarer's orders, not to be disturbed by anyone. (Truth, GM: Ivro coughed blood in the night; Hannet dosed him with poppy at dawn; he's asleep. IVRO'S HEALTH -> 2/4.) Tobin writes her visit into his book as soon as she turns away.
- Caria found the search. Sulphur reagent confiscated -> Poison Cloud full ritual now lacks a key reagent: GM ruling +2 DL (or impossible if no substitute) until she finds sulphur (apothecary in Crossroads; hot springs; a smith's forge-flux).
- Her rage: the dead wick on Asher's candle glowed ember-red for a heartbeat as she fumed (aura; harmless; no witness).
- Scene 6: the chapel, midday. Kade is there, kneeling at the back; the grey hound lies at his feet in the Borderlands (Caria can *sense* a cold, patient spirit presence with base Conjuror perception, without the Eye). Kade wants to take her measure before the road.
- Retro roll: Kade's hound noticed Asher leave last night (3 vs 1, effect 2): Kade knows a small fire spirit left from the kitchen wing at dusk and went east along the hearths toward the coast. He suspects it's hers; not certain.
- Kade (@@*): pragmatic professional, not a zealot. Finds lost things for the Almoner - currently the Hierarch's "property" (the ghola). Suspects something is wrong in the west wing; keeps his head down and his hands clean. Respects craft. Caria's word "project" snagged his attention.
- Caria's first-principles tutor (@@*): Magistra Oriane Vesk - one of the Atlantean Sorcerer's early students at the Ganth Temple; taught in a back room there until the Temple "decided it didn't care for her." Current whereabouts: TBD (GM). Kade knew of her by sight years ago.
- Kade to Caria: never say "what might burn" where Calder can hear it.
- Scene 7: kitchens, late afternoon. Mags gives provisions (bread, hard cheese, dried apples, small jar of honey "for the shrine-keeper") and a contact: her late brother's widow, Berra, cooks at the Crossroads Inn & Tavern - "tell her Mags sent you and she'll feed you and keep her mouth shut."
- ASHER'S TRIP (rolled): discretion 0 vs Shimmer 2 -> Shimmer caught him. She doused him (Complication 2 on Asher: small, sputtering, damp-smoky). He sensed nothing definite near the Teeth (1 vs DL1 tie) - Shimmer chased him off before he got close.
- What he learned (to report): villagers' mood - defiant, close-knit, proud of standing up to the priest, watching every stranger; warehouse full of tarred nets and dry rope (burns); thatched huts around the lodge (burn); lodge hall of timber and stone (half); Karinna's hut/Susheil shrine - a deep, old, WET anchor, strong; he couldn't get near it.
- WHAT HE LET SLIP to Shimmer (lovesick): that a friend from "the mountain house" sent him, "the one with the white in her hair," and that she's coming with the wagons. He did NOT say "Child of the Contract."
- CONSEQUENCE in Saltwind: Shimmer tells Mira; Min also felt a fire-spirit pass. Saltwind now expects a white-haired woman from the monastery who sent a fire-demon to scout "what burns." Karinna's "spies in the caravan" warning looks confirmed. Caria will arrive into suspicion.
- Asher returns the second night, as promised. Payment due: one secret, told to the flame.
- Caria drew Asher close and shared warmth freely in the Borderlands. His Complication clears (care = recovery). Under the Accords he ACCEPTED a comfort -> small favor on his side: the ledger tips toward him owing her a little. He knows it; so does she.
- PAYMENT: Caria told Asher a never-told secret (her feelings for Merrit; fear her curse drives it; fear of hurting him). Proper payment, accepted -> the scouting Agreement is concluded and wiped clean. Remaining balance: Asher owes her a small favor (the accepted comfort).
- Asher's reaction: a flare of jealous blue, then smaller and honest. Private GM note: his Hook (Smitten) is now tangled with a rival. He will not tell anyone this secret (he promised unprompted - spirits keep promises).

## Between S01 and S02
- Caria: Conjuror 2 (extreme), Performer 1 / Mediation 1, 1 Karma carried. Lee has NOT read the ledger; keep secrets.
- Asher now Level 2 (True Name 2). Hook A Hint of Brimstone 2 - may pay Karma twice per session.

## SESSION 2 - started 2026-09-27 evening (Lee's clock)
- Caria: +1 attendance Karma -> 2 unspent. Luck Tokens reset 2/2. Hook (rank 2) may pay Karma twice this session.
- GM Tokens reset: 3/3.
- Scene 1: predawn, her cell. Morning prayers with Asher as the altar-light (he is Level 2 now - steadier, brighter).
- Caria recast Fax Iacta, Improvised Persistent + Guidable (Difficulty 2), EL5 -> rolled 1 (would lose the spell!). She spent a LUCK TOKEN (pre-declared intent) -> best result 5; holds. Effect 3: a clean blue-green flame two inches off the brass cap of her staff. Tokens 1/2.
- GM ruling: Persistent on a self-carried light lasts until she sleeps or dismisses it (not merely the scene).
- Persistent per Lee: lasts until there's a reason it shouldn't; brightness adjustable (torch/lantern).
- Asher invited to ride in the Fax flame on her staff - ACCEPTED. Under the Accords, hospitality accepted = another small favor on his side (he owes her a bit more). Visible sign: the blue-green flame now has a gold heart. Anyone with Second Sight (Kade, his hound) will see a spirit riding her staff.
- Asher's choice on the invitation: "Both. So neither." - hospitality and company cancel; no new debt. (He still owes her the earlier small favor.)
- Scene 2: the North Road below the monastery gate, dawn. Caria Tokens refill -> 2/2. GM 3/3.
- Dasha Korrin (@@*): fifties, weathered, grey braid, driving whip coiled on her shoulder; 11 wagons (grain, salt fish, bolt cloth, iron nails, pottery); 6 hired guards (spear and cudgel, one crossbow), drovers. Hates the monastery's road-tax. Sees a monastery robe and assumes "collector" until shown otherwise.
- Kade already seated on wagon 3 (hound under the axle). Fennick on wagon 4 with a lap-desk and tally sticks.
- Bargain with Korrin: Caria rides free and eats with the drovers in exchange for keeping her light at night camps and spirit-watching past the fey ruin beyond the Crossroads fork (oxen balk there; a drover's boy walked into the trees last spring and never returned). Warnings go to Korrin only.
- Caria, looking at wagon 4: "if I see a thief among your goods I will set him aflame." Fennick heard it and wrote it down. (Evidence for the Almoner. Clock stays 3/4 for now; the NEXT reportable offense ticks it to 4/4.)
- Seating: Caria rides wagon 1 with Korrin (duffel under the canvas behind the seat). Kade wagon 3, Fennick wagon 4.
- Route/timing: Monastery -> Crossroads by nightfall (Crossroads Inn; Berra). Day 2: Crossroads -> the fork -> past the fey ruin -> Saltwind (long day; possibly a night camp near the ruin).
- Caria relayed Kade's warning to Korrin verbatim. Korrin: last spring's lost drover's boy, Jem (14), told the others the night before he vanished that he'd heard his mother calling from the trees near the ruin - his mother died two winters ago. Nobody listened.
- GM (@@*): TWO threats on this road. (1) The ghola - Calder's lost "property," flesh-eater, somewhere between the ruin and the coast. (2) The fey ruin by the fork - a troll-built estate that follows dream logic; a corrupted fey remnant ("the Caller") lures lone watchers with beloved voices. They are NOT the same thing; Kade's warning happens to cover both.
- Korrin orders the doubled watch and "nobody leaves the firelight alone" down the line.
- Caria told Kade about Jem. Kade: timing is wrong for his quarry - the ghola has been loose for weeks, not since spring. Something ELSE on this road calls with borrowed voices. He's professionally interested. Advice: at the ruin, don't open the Eye - whatever's there will see her looking.
- Crossroads smith (@@*): Garrow Hask - fifties, bald, scorched forearms, leather apron, devotee of Carterion (a small iron-and-bronze Carterion icon over the forge). Wary of monastery robes, respects craft and fire. Keeps flux, borax, and a crock of yellow sulphur for brazing and for fumigating hives - he'll sell a twist.
- Forge spirit (@@*): an old, grumpy fire spirit living in Garrow's forge for generations ("Old Bellows" to Garrow, who half-believes in it). Noticed Asher at once; the forge fire leaned toward the door.
- Caria offered Old Bellows a favor of his choosing, EXPLICITLY excluding any return but the right to say she'd done it (no debt created - clean Accords terms; Bellows approved).
- Bellows's request: carry a live coal of his fire to the sea and give it to the water, so that Old Bellows can say he has touched the sea - something he's wanted for three hundred years. The coal must be kept alive on the road (Garrow will give her a clay fire-pot with a lid). (@@* GM: at Saltwind, that means offering fire into Shimmer's domain. Could be an insult or a courtesy depending entirely on how it's done.)
- Bought from Garrow for 2 silver (no haggling): amber (quail-egg size, a winged thing inside - a Fax Iacta binding reagent), twist of flowers of brimstone (Poison Cloud reagent RESTORED - the +2 Difficulty is gone), sack of applewood charcoal. Plus the fire-pot with Bellows's coal and a sack of forge charcoal (gifts). Caria's coin: savings reduced by 2 silver (@@ remaining amount unspecified - "small").
- Garrow will send word of interesting goods: "Caria of Green Haven, at the Monastery." His late wife's sister lives in Green Haven.
- Crossroads shrine cluster: neglected this season. The circuit priest hasn't come in two months (monastery-appointed circuit disrupted). Offerings sparse. Rikitak's niche defaced (horns scratched) - local resentment of the monastery. To her spirit-sense, the Crossroads convergence is strong but THIN, like a river running low and too fast - worn channels, less water. (GM: corrupted currents; do not name the cause.)
- Offerings (bowls cleaned first): Grolikarnigan - dried apple; Susheil - hard brandy from her flask; Rana - bread for the birds; Carterion - a pinch of Garrow's applewood charcoal (Lee: meant to buy lamp oil too; no oil on her).
- Rikitak: duplicate Fax Iacta, Improvised Persistent, into his defaced bowl. EL5 rolled 1 vs Difficulty 1 = tie -> hold FAILS (would destabilize Fax, killing the staff light and evicting Asher). Lee spent a LUCK TOKEN ex post facto and insisted -> best result 5, holds. Not an Extreme (GM ruling, UNCOMMITTED). Caria Tokens 1/2.
- RESULT: a fuelless blue-white flame burns in Rikitak's bowl all night, beside the scratched-out horns. Staff light (with Asher) unaffected. (@@* GM: townsfolk WILL notice at dawn. Who lit the demon-god's bowl the night the monastery girl passed through? Reaction TBD - could cut either way given local resentment.)

### S02 resumed 2026-09-28 morning (Lee's clock)
- Scene 4: Crossroads Inn, kitchen door, dusk. New scene -> Caria Tokens refill 1 -> 2/2. GM 3/3 (full).

- Dog (@@*): "Abbot" - old grey lurcher, named by Berra's late husband "because he eats everything, does nothing, and sleeps on the best blanket." Tolerant of Asher; curious about the fire-pot (Bellows's coal).

- Caria strips robe, sash, breastplate; folds into satchel under the table beside Abbot (he allows it, rests his chin on it); parazonium on top. - LEE CANON (setting etiquette, Order of Rikitak): never ASK a priest of the Order for help - tell him someone is hurt or sick; he should always come on his own, and never ask a price you cannot pay. Asking = requesting aid = a price is owed ("fire teaches").
- Caria quizzes Dunny on why. Dunny's answer: "'Cause if you ask, it's a bargain. And you don't bargain with fire." (Folk-level Accords wisdom.)

## SESSION 3 - started 2026-09-28 midday (Lee's clock)
- Caria: +1 attendance -> 4 Karma (carry cap applies at session end). Luck Tokens 2/2. Hook (rank 2) may pay Karma twice. GM Tokens 3/3.
- Wearing yellow Shrinekeeper robes. Not yet in orange.
- GM HIDDEN (decided before the fork): the Caller, burned off Caria, reaches instead for CLERK FENNICK. (@@*) Fennick's secret: a younger sister, Ada, dead of fever at nine when he was fourteen; he still carries her little tin whistle. He is lonely and exact, and the tally-work is how he copes. The Caller will use Ada's voice (and the whistle) tonight if the caravan camps within its reach. Signs today: Fennick uncharacteristically distracted, loses count, hums.
- The ghola (Calder's "property") is still ahead, between the ruin and the coast. Kade on wagon 3 is hunting it.
- Route today: Crossroads -> the fork (late morning) -> past the fey ruin (afternoon) -> Korrin wants to camp at the old ford beyond the ruin (@@* "Tanner's Ford") before dark. Saltwind tomorrow midday.
- Scene 1: wagon 1, rolling south, morning.
- Caria sends Asher to look at Fennick. Tells Korrin: "if I find he is to blame, in whole or in part, for this lapse, I will do my duty" (double-edged, deliberately). Asks to step down and walk back along the line.
- Korrin: fine, they're at a walking pace; "Don't set my clerk alight before Saltwind. He's paid through."
- ROLL: Asher reads Fennick EL2 -> 0 1 = 1 vs Diff 1 -> TIE, fails. Asher does NOT sense the Caller's residue. Reports only the mundane: Fennick can't see spirits (didn't notice him); he's off today - lost his count twice, humming a little tune under his breath, keeps touching something in his coat's breast pocket. "Just a sad little man."
- GM HIDDEN: the Caller DID taste Fennick last night at the inn (his room was under the loft; Berra's silver not yet laid). The tune he's humming is the one Ada used to play on the tin whistle. The residue is there for Caria's own Eye / Second Sight (Conjuror EL4) if she looks.
- (Lee: "I'll be right back" was Caria, in character.)
- Korrin on payment: the Almoner's office paid Fennick's and Kade's passage up front, in coin - "the only bill your mountain pays on time is for moving its own."
- Caria walks back to wagon 3: "Good morning, master Kade. May I?"
- Kade's spirit-hound (@@*): "Hollis" - grey, long-legged, visible to ordinary eyes as a grey lurcher-ish dog, to Second Sight as smoke with eyes. Last night past midnight Hollis woke Kade with hackles up, staring at the inn roof; then turned and stared down the south road toward the fork for an hour. Kade will share this and ask Caria whether she dreamed.
- Caria tells Kade the whole dream, in detail (infirmary, Aubric's voice and words, the blue-white fire, the hall/fountain/trees, the thing; the loft, JEM and the carved bird, the mice, Jem's wren-song dream per Dunny).
- Kade's read (@@*): the hall is the old estate past the fork - drovers call it "the Weeping Court" (a Sarina-era fey house, troll-built; the fountain ran once). The thing is a corrupted remnant: a pure-spirit fae whose anchor was broken, starved and twisted by Death magic; it feeds on longing. It cannot take a sleeper from a bed - it needs the victim to WALK to it. Dream = the hook; voice from the trees near the ruin = the reel. Mice going silent is how you know it came close.
- Kade on the blue fire: not Asher's, not hers - "someone with a claim on you was paying attention. That's very good or very bad, and with gods it's usually both."
- Kade's pointed question: it didn't get her, so it went looking elsewhere under that roof - "who else from this caravan slept at the inn last night?" (Answer: Fennick. Kade does not know Fennick had a room; Caria does.)
- Caria returns to Korrin to ask who slept at the inn. (OOC: Lee asked whether Caria knows. She knew Fennick took a room - she saw him paying, heard his voice through the wall, and was told the room was under the loft. She doesn't know about anyone else.)
- Korrin: only two from the caravan paid for beds - Korrin (front room over the taproom) and "your clerk" (back of the house, next to the kitchen chimney). Everyone else slept with the wagons in the yard. Kade too, with Hollis.
- Korrin: no dreams - "I sleep like the dead, and the dead don't dream, Sister."
- Asher ordered to shadow Fennick, alert on any Working or any move to leave the wagons. Asher: yes, fiercely.
- ROLL: Caria feels the road ahead, EL4 -> 1 0 0 1 = 2 vs Diff 1 -> effect 1. She feels the Crossroads current running south, thin and fast, and past the fork bending off into the trees like water toward a drain. And a thin cold thread from somewhere behind wagon 1, running south toward that bend. She can't pinpoint whose (effect 1).
- Caria plans a Ward for Fennick; walking back to Kade. Lee asked for an inventory (given OOC). NOTE: Korrin's cargo includes IRON NAILS (cold iron) and salt fish.
- Caria's read of the thread (her training, no roll): a HOOK/tether, not an anchor. An anchor is a spirit's own seat (place/thing it dwells in); this is a line run out from elsewhere into a person.
- Kade on specificity: commit to FEY. "It keeps fey rules - it can't take you, you must go to it; it needs you to say yes. Death hollowed it; it didn't make it." Iron for the fey. Also: "The door is whatever he misses. Close the door and the hook has nothing to pull on." Suggests Korrin's nails.
- @@ UNCOMMITTED (asked Lee): specificity in Wards - does naming the spirit's nature correctly give a Boost, and naming it wrongly a penalty?
- Rules settled OOC (see gap log): keyed Ward = wager capped at Role rank (Caria: 2 vs fey); duration = until there's a reason it shouldn't; iron = Protected N vs fey (GM sets N by quantity/arrangement), needs a successful base roll, stacks with a keyed wager.
- Caria asks Kade: better for him to persuade Fennick to accept her Ward, or Korrin to lend iron? Kade: he'll take Fennick (same master - the Almoner's office; Fennick will hear it from a colleague, not from the Sister he's writing up). Caria takes Korrin ("she likes you, heaven knows why"). Kade will also try to learn what Fennick misses. Kade's caution: "Don't tell him what's behind the door. A grieving man will fight you to keep it open."
- Caria glances for the Arch of Heaven through the trees; a private prayer ("Expectations are a fool's errand. I serve, my Lord.").
- Back on wagon 1, standing on the bench (Korrin allows it, one hand on her belt). SECOND SIGHT over the caravan: EL4 -> 1 1 1 0 = 3 vs Diff 2 -> effect 1. She sees:
  - The thread: fine, cold, grey-silver, set into FENNICK at his breast pocket - the hook is in the THING he keeps touching, not in him directly. Runs off south-west over the trees toward the bend in the current.
  - Asher: a gold spark riding the wagon-4 canvas above Fennick, bristling.
  - Kade: aura banked tight; Hollis smoke-with-eyes; Kade glances up at her as she looks - he knows.
  - Wagon 2 (@@*): the iron-nail load shows as a dull dead lump in the Borderlands, a hole in the colors. So the nails are on wagon 2, not wagon 1.
  - Drovers, guards, oxen: ordinary warm colors. Nothing else hooked.
- Caria lets the Sight fade, sits: "I don't think I've ever seen that much cold iron in one spot." Korrin (@@*): the nails are the most valuable load in the train - boat-nails for the Saltwind shipwrights, ordered a year ahead, bought dear from a Carterion-licensed ironmonger in the north; counted by the keg (six kegs), and Korrin answers for every one. Wary, but curious why a Sister cares.
- Caria tells Korrin frankly: the thing on the road has latched onto the clerk ("malicious unseelie"); she wants him sitting ON the iron, strapped in if possible. No roll - Korrin lost Jem last spring on this road and is motivated.
- Korrin's solutions (@@*): (1) No strapping a monastery clerk to her cargo - "that's the kind of thing your Almoner hears about." (2) The trick: she'll ask Fennick to re-count and re-seal-check the six nail kegs on wagon 2 "for the Saltwind manifest" - he'll sit on those kegs all afternoon happily auditing. (3) Big drover Tam (@@*, wagon 2's driver) rides beside him. (4) Tonight at Tanner's Ford: the six kegs come off wagon 2 and ring his bedroll, seals intact - she won't open them.
- Iron value (GM): kegs sealed, wood between - still iron. Riding on wagon 2 = Protected 1 vs fey. Six kegs ringed round a bedroll = Protected 3 vs fey (@@* GM-set per Lee's ruling).
- Iron values revised per Lee: wagon 2 ride, passive = Protected 1, circumventable by lure. Six sealed kegs worked into Caria's Ward tonight = +3 to the Ward vs fey (near-full value), and the Caller must batter the Ward head-on.

Korrin: goes grey, then iron-hard; passes the word down the line herself at the next water stop - "nobody answers any voice from the trees, not even a boy's; you hear Jem, you grab the nearest man and sit on him." (Caria's warning = inoculation for Korrin's own door. @@* GM: Korrin now resistant - +1 vs the Caller's lure.)
- Lee idea: every man with a silver coin in his teeth tonight. GM notes: silver is scarce among drovers (copper wages); Korrin's trade purse and Fennick's office purse are the likely sources. - Caria to Asher: she'll arrange something to make him happy enough to owe her (offers her Oath). "If I owe you a favor so that you will owe me, who owes whom?" Asher: delighted - "Both. So neither.
