Old Bellows?) may see it on her.
- Caria wonders how Kade knew "seven": Korrin's voice carries - "seven short" was heard three wagons back. Kade: "Half the line heard. Your caravan mistress has a voice made for calling oxen."
- "May he deliver it in good health." Kade's mouth twitches.
- Late morning: the FORK comes into view ahead.
- Timing (@@* GM): the fork at late morning (~11th hour). Fork -> Tanner's Ford about 14 miles; oxen ~2-2.5 mph with a noon water stop -> arrive ~6 pm. Sunset ~7 pm (early autumn). About an hour of slack. The road skirts the Weeping Court woods for ~3 miles, roughly 1-3 pm (closest ~2 pm; the ruin itself a mile into the trees). Tanner's Ford is ~5 miles SE of the ruin as the crow flies - well within the Caller's reach along the current.
- Fennick already moved to wagon 2 at the pre-fork water stop (Korrin's "re-count the kegs for the Saltwind manifest" worked). He sits on the nail kegs with his lap-desk; Tam drives. Asher moved with him (gold spark on wagon 2's canvas).
- Caria hops down to walk beside wagon 2 and talk to Fennick ("harder to think of someone as the enemy when they are present"). Scene 2: wagon 2, the road south of the fork, late morning.
- Caria climbs aboard wagon 2; greets Tam (he ducks his head, reddens, "Sister"); courteous to Fennick, offers not to interrupt. Fennick disarmed by the courtesy; admits he's counted keg three four times and keeps losing the tally. Asks for the particulars now.
- Caria tells Fennick the truth against Kade's advice (fey + demonic taint; iron and silver; dream, then the voice; it came to her first and was rebuffed; "You will almost certainly hear the voice. It is not the one you loved."; Korrin likely to hear Jem; it wants to feed; his grief is the gravy).
- ROLL: Mediation EL4 -> 0 0 1 0 = 1 vs Fennick's denial EL3 -> 0 1 1 = 2. FAILS pending Lee's Token decision (Caria 2/2).
- If it stands: Fennick goes cold and proper; accepts the Ward procedurally (Kade's deal holds) but takes the telling as an intrusion; hand clamps on the pocket; now he's LISTENING for the voice - Kade's warning bites (the Caller's lure tonight +1 vs him).
- Lee adds the HOOK (A Hint of Brimstone 2) to the honesty roll after the fact: +2 dice -> 0 0. Total 6 dice: 0 0 1 0 0 0 = 1 vs Fennick 2. STILL FAILS. Susceptibility test answer: Fennick is NOT open to Caria's glamour - his distrust of the demon-marked is armor against HER specifically (not against grief).
- OPTICS COST: he smelled it - struck match, faint heat. Pending Token: if the roll stands, he writes "Sister's address accompanied by a sulphurous odour" in his book (evidence for the Almoner; latent Purification weight, not an immediate tick). The Hook's taint derails her plan to win his trust -> HOOK TRIGGER, +1 Karma (first this session) @@ offered to Lee, vetoable.
- Lee spends a LUCK TOKEN on the honesty roll -> best result 6 vs Fennick 2 -> effect 4. Caria Tokens 1/2. Hook Karma stands (Lee didn't veto; "relieved" = the in-fiction answer that Fennick isn't glamour-susceptible to her). Caria Karma 5 (4 + Hook).
- Effect 4: her honesty lands. Fennick doesn't write the sulphur line (he remembers the smell). He answers the pocket question: a small dented tin whistle, blue paint mostly worn off - ADA's. She was nine, fever, the winter he was fourteen and away at the monastery school; he didn't get home in time. He's dreamed of her playing it since the inn. The tune he hums is hers.
- Fennick holds the whistle out on his palm - not quite offering it. The hook's thread is set in the WHISTLE (Caria's Sight confirms if she looks).
- Caria folds Fennick's hand shut over the whistle; "Grief is just love with nowhere left to go... how dare that THING touch her memory"; tears; "You have will. Guard her. Do not give her to it."
- No roll (follows the effect-4 success). RESULT: Fennick's longing turns to protectiveness - the door's hinge changes. Named Persistent Boost for Fennick tonight: "NOT HERS TO TAKE" +1 vs the Caller's lure (cancels the earlier "listening" risk, which never applied since the Token won).
- Fennick: "I do not see you as a child, Sister. I see someone who has buried people." Keeps the whistle - but asks whether she can keep it from IT without keeping it from HIM. (Opening to ward the whistle itself.)
- Tam, driving, doesn't turn round; wipes his face on his sleeve once.
- Caria offers to hold the whistle, perhaps exorcise the influence. Fennick agrees - UNTIL DAWN - and writes a receipt ("Received in trust of R. Fennick, clerk: one tin whistle, formerly blue, dented. To be returned at dawn. Signed: ___") and asks her to sign. She is now holding the hook-point.
- When she takes it: the cold thread runs through her palm, taut south-west. It twitches once - tasting who holds it - recognizes her (the burned hand) and goes STILL. Not slack: waiting. (@@* GM: the Caller won't pull on it while she holds it; it will wait for her to put it down, fall asleep, or be distracted. Or it will try elsewhere - Korrin, or someone who takes a coin out of his teeth.)
- Caria signs the receipt with her full title, no flourish.
- ROLL: reading the thread, testing hairpin silver + keg iron: EL5 -> 1 1 1 1 1 = 5 -> EXTREME SUCCESS. Free Token (1 -> 2/2). 1 Karma earmarked to CONJUROR (Conj 2->3 costs 2 and needs Level 3 first; this is 1 toward it - flag at session end).
- She learns (effect 3 + extreme):
  - The hook's hold = 3 (@@* GM stat). The Caller: roughly Level 4, starved, Complication 1 (burned, from S2). Not stronger than her Ward + iron + wager if she's ready.
  - IRON: near the kegs the thread goes brittle and stiff, like frozen line - it can't pull *through* iron; a lure can't cross an iron boundary cleanly.
  - SILVER: touched with the hairpin, the thread blackens and smokes where it touches - silver burns the rot; it weakens the hold itself. Both work, differently (confirms the canon).
  - EXTREME side effect: along the taut line, a glimpse back to its source - the dry fountain, the hollow thing - and OTHER threads running from it. Most are old, dead, cut, trailing like rotten string. ONE is not dead: thin as a hair, and warm. (GM HIDDEN @@*: JEM. Alive, dream-held in the Weeping Court since spring - fey-time; the Caller feeds on his longing slowly, the way it meant to feed on Fennick. Rescue is possible. Kade doesn't know.)
- REBUKE down the line: Caria invokes iron + silver, "GODS DAMN THEE, IN HEAVEN'S NAME!!" aloud. EL7 (Lv2+Conj2+iron kegs invoked 2+silver in reach 1) -> 1 0 0 0 1 1 1 = 4. Caller opposes EL3 (Lv4 -1 burned) -> 0 1 1 = 2. Lee pre-declared a TOKEN -> 7 vs 2 = EFFECT 5. Caria Tokens 1/2. GM Tokens NOT spent (it would not change the outcome category - Down either way; saving them).
- RESULT: Caller takes 5 spiritual Harm (Complication) on top of 1 -> 6 > Level 4 -> DOWN. It stays Down until Harm drops below 4 (needs to shed 3). GM: spirits recover ~1 per day undisturbed -> DOWN FOR ABOUT 3 DAYS. (@@* recovery rate)
- The line burns out end to end - Fennick's hook SEVERED. The whistle is clean.
- The Caller, Down, has retreated deep into the Court - and it clings to its one live thread (Jem) harder, feeding to heal. (@@* GM: Jem's clock starts. Rescue window = while it's Down, ~3 days; but its feeding while hurt drains Jem faster. JEM CLOCK 0/4, ticks each day; at 4 he's gone.)
- Witnesses: Tam yelled and nearly lost the reins; oxen shied; Fennick dropped his pen. The whole back half of the caravan heard the Sister scream a curse "in Heaven's name." Kade + Hollis felt it like a thunderclap.
- Caria returns the whistle to Fennick, promises a candle for Ada tonight, kisses his head, leaps down. Fennick: holds the whistle in both hands; says nothing; (@@* he writes nothing about the curse. He will later write "Sister Greenhaven: see separate report," and the separate report will be favorable. Latent.)
- Caria calls Kade to go NOW and kill it before it feeds. She believes the warm thread is "the last anchor" - she does NOT know it's a person (Jem).
- Kade: IN, with conditions. (1) Not from here - the Court is 4+ miles through trees from this point. Ride on with the caravan to the woods-stretch (~2 pm), where the Court is a mile in; go from there. (2) Korrin must be told; the caravan keeps to its road and goes on to the Ford. They rejoin at Tanner's Ford on foot (~5 miles SE of the Court) - dusk at 7. (3) "Down is not dead. And the house is worse than its tenant." Hollis goes with them. (4) Kade notes dryly the whole caravan heard her curse.
- Time now ~noon, approaching the Hollow water stop.
- Caria goes to Korrin to explain. Korrin speaks first ("You screamed at the sky, Sister.") Hears it: the thing is Down; Caria and Kade go in at the woods-stretch and walk out to the Ford. Korrin releases her from the watch for the afternoon; the caravan goes on. Korrin BREAKS ONE KEG SEAL herself and gives a double fistful of ship-nails (~40, cold-wrought) in a sacking bag: "You're not going into that house without iron." (Iron: Protected 2 vs fey carried, per ruling; more if worked into a Ward.) Asks, not looking at her: "If there's anything of the boy in there... bring it out." (Caria doesn't know about the warm thread = Jem.)
- ~2 hours until the woods-stretch. Noon water stop at the Hollow coming up.
- Caria reasons it through (Halfling? possession? manifest + anchor?). Talks it over with Kade walking the woods-stretch after the Hollow noon stop.
- GM DECISION (hidden, @@*): The Caller is a PURE-SPIRIT high-fey remnant. Its ANCHOR is the Court's heart - the dry fountain (every fey house was built around a heart). Its long grey form is a Physical Manifestation (persistent wagered Boost). Down = manifestation broken; it has retreated INTO the anchor under the fountain and will re-manifest when it recovers (~3 days, faster if it feeds on Jem). NOT a Halfling; NOT a possession. To end it: exorcise it while Down AND break/cleanse/salt the anchor, or it returns.
- Kade's contribution: "Not a Halfling. The Halflings were the rebels; this is what they rebelled against." "If it had a body it would have walked to Crossroads itself. It needs YOU to walk." Every fey house has a heart - "the fountain, I'd wager. Break the heart, or salt it and iron it, and it has nowhere to come home to." Asks what else she saw down the line.
- Caria tells Kade: maybe the anchor line, but she thinks it has another victim STASHED and went to feed. Kade connects it: fey keep sleepers; fey-time runs slow in those houses; "Korrin's boy went in last spring." If it's feeding to heal, the sleeper is ALIVE - "we're not only hunting now, we're fetching." Don't tell Korrin until they know. Waking a fey-held sleeper is its own danger (dream-logic; the sleeper may not want to wake) - Healer work as much as Conjuror.
- ~2 pm: Hollis stops at a deer-track running west into the holly; the point where the Court is a mile in. Caravan rolls on toward the Ford.
- Caria assigns Asher the caravan's bonfire tonight: keep it bright, burn anything coming in that shouldn't, singe anyone leaving who shouldn't. Asher: protests once ("I should be WITH you in that house"), then obeys - and reminds her she carries Old Bellows's live coal: "You're not going in there without fire." He goes up the line to the caravan. Caria is WITHOUT Asher in the Court (no Query the Candle there).
- Caria shouts ahead to Korrin: "Coins and nails, and EARNEST prayers!" Korrin, far up the line, raises the whip overhead in answer.
- Caria torn between the caravan and the Court. At the holly gap with Kade and Hollis, ~2 pm.
- Scene 4: into the woods toward the Weeping Court, ~2 pm. New scene -> Caria Tokens 1 -> 2/2. GM 3/3.
- Caria feeds Bellows's fire-pot (forge charcoal). Fear, unimpeded. No light needed yet: green gloom under oak/beech/holly, walkable.
- Walk (no roll; Hollis leads): birdsong thins then stops ~half a mile in. Old moss-grown boundary stones, fey-carved. At ~3/4 mile: the Court's outer wall comes into view through the trees - troll-built, too tall, dark stone, a GATE-ARCH with no gate, and beside it a door set into the wall three feet above the ground, opening onto nothing.
- Caria asks Kade about Second Sight here. Kade: the tenant is Down, so the old warning (it would see her looking) is weaker - but THE HOUSE notices. "In a place like this the Eye shows what's true, and the house hates being seen true. Glance. Don't stare." (@@* GM: Sight here reveals which doors/paths are real; each use of the Eye inside the Court ticks a HOUSE-AWARENESS clock 0/4. At 4 the house starts rearranging against them.)
- Kade takes a handful of nails (Protected 2 vs fey for him too). Ties one to Hollis's collar with a thong - Hollis is a spirit-hound but NOT fey; iron is nothing to him. Hollis sniffs it with visible disdain.
- HOUSE ACTIVATION replaces the House-Awareness clock (Lee design). Proposed start: Activation 1 (the Rebuke rattled it). Sight successes bank as Activation dice (unless wagered "quietly"); the house rolls Activation vs her perception/navigation; when it wins it can lie. Lulling (Conjuror / Performer lullaby / offerings) subtracts.
- CORRECTION (Lee canon, 2026-09-28 late): a manifest spirit whose physical form is forcibly and violently disrupted is automatically Down/Staggered and EJECTED from the local Borderlands - banished to the far Dreamlands - until summoned back by explicit ritual or by an anchor. Neither can happen until it has recovered (Harm below Level). SUPERSEDES "retreated into its anchor under the fountain." The fountain-anchor still matters: it's the Caller's way BACK once recovered.
- Caller Harm tally: 1 (burn, S2) + 5 (Rebuke) = 6 vs Level 4. Harm TYPE not yet fixed - see gap log recovery analysis. @@ OPEN (Lee): does the Jem thread survive the banishment (the Dreamlands is where dreamers are)?
- Lee procedure: a post-combat recovery roll decides how much of the Harm was only stun (Complication) and how much was Injury. ROLL: Caller base Lv4, no bonuses -> 0 0 1 0 = 1. So 1 of 6 was stun; INJURY 5 remains (vs Level 4).
- Death threshold: none in the book. GM proposed BEFORE rolling: a spirit whose remaining Injury EXCEEDS its Level is "dead" - dispersed, reforming in some nether, a spiritual coma of perhaps centuries. By that reading the Caller (Injury 5 > 4) is effectively gone. Alternative: dead only at a higher threshold, in which case it's Down with Injury 5, can't recover unaided (EL4 can't beat 5), and must FEED. PENDING Lee's ruling.
- Lee: if Jem is in a SIDHE (a fey pocket-realm beneath the Court), he's effectively reachable from the Dreamlands - the thread survives the banishment. GM adopts: Jem is held in a sidhe under the Court.
- Repo check: Lee reports his repo (esp. the magick section) looks like an unfinished merge. The session's clone = origin/main @ 4dff37b (2026-09-18), clean, no conflict markers, no duplicated lines in magick/*.md. So the damage is in Lee's local working copy (or unpushed/another branch) - not visible from here unless he links the folder.
- LEE RULING: the Caller is DEAD (Injury 5 > Level 4) - dispersed, reforming in some nether, a spiritual coma of generations. GM keeps wriggle-room. It is out of this story.
- GM HIDDEN (@@*), what that means now: the SIDHE under the Court was the Caller's larder; untended, its dream is starting to fray. The HOUSE (genius loci, Activation 1) has kept Jem as its last guest and is lonely; it doesn't want to let him go, and it can lie. Jem is dreaming a spring night at home (his mother singing the wren song). Fey-time: for him it's been one long night. Waking him inside a fraying sidhe is Healer + Conjuror work; bringing him out means persuading or out-lying the house. His body: thin, cold, alive - fey keeping, not starvation. The old victims' remains (the dead threads) lie in the Court's lower rooms.
- Caria plans a disguise: (1) gather fey-aspected mana from the ley drain as a Boost; (2) a Persistent wagered Boost "unnoticed by fey" for her Sight rolls in the Court.
- ROLL (1): gather, EL4 vs Diff 1 -> 1 0 0 1 = 2 -> effect 1 -> +1 fey-aspected Boost for the disguise roll. No Activation cost (drawn outside the wall, from the current, not the house).
- (2) pending: Lee asked about the Hook and the odds. GM answers: the Hook applies (tapping the Borderlands; once per scene, unused this scene), BUT it's brimstone - if the roll fails, the brimstone is what the house notices (+1 Activation). Opposition = the house's Activation (1 die). Wager cap = Conjuror 2. EL 2+2+1(+2 Hook) = 5 (7) - 2 wagered -> roll 3 (5) dice vs 1d2. P(win) ~69% without the Hook, ~89% with it; the Token makes it certain. Effect if it holds: named Persistent Boost "Borrowed Fey-Stink" (@@* name) = 2; up to 2 successes per Sight use DON'T bank as Activation. Lasts the scene (or until there's a reason it shouldn't).
- ROLL (2) disguise, no Hook: 3 dice -> 0 0 0 = EXTREME FAILURE. House Activation 1d -> 0. Pending Lee's Token (Caria 2/2).
- If it stands (GM): the scummed mana curdles in her hands - the house TASTES someone working its own current at its door. Activation 1 -> 3. Caria takes Complication 1 ("sorrow-sick": a wave of the house's grief, nausea, tears). AND the house now knows her flavor: it thinks she's a guest arriving. (@@* it will try to make her welcome - hospitality as a trap.)
- LEE RULING: a Token spent against an EXTREME FAILURE only makes it a COMMON failure. Lee spends it. Caria Tokens 1/2. Disguise FAILS normally: the scum Boost is spent and wasted, no blind, no curdling, no Complication, Activation stays 1 (zero successes bank nothing).
- Disguise RETRY (dropping the tainted scum; allowed as a changed approach): EL4 - 2 wagered -> 2 dice: 0 1 = 1 vs house Activation 1d: 1 = 1. TIE -> house holds, pending Lee: Hook as +2 flat successes (-> 3 vs 1, succeeds; a faint brimstone note in the blind), or the last Token (-> 2 vs 1).
- Lee uses the HOOK (+2 flat successes, after the fact) on the retry: 1+2 = 3 vs house 1 -> effect 2. Blind HOLDS. Hook spent for Scene 4.
- Named Persistent Boost: "HUNTER'S BLIND" (@@* name), rating 2 (the wager), keyed to fey noticing. Up to 2 successes per Sight use inside the Court don't bank as Activation. Lasts the scene or until there's a reason it shouldn't. House Activation stays 1.
- Hook bill (latent, @@* GM): a thread of brimstone is woven into the blind. Fey won't see HER looking - but anything in the Court that knows the smell of demons will find the blind itself a curiosity. Not a trigger now (voluntary use; no plan derailed).
- "We're coming, Jem." SECOND SIGHT on the painted door: EL4 -> 0 0 0 0 = EXTREME FAILURE. House Activation 1d -> 1. House wins; it LIES. Zero successes, so no Activation banks (the Blind doesn't matter).
- GM TRUTH (hidden): the little painted door is the REAL way down - the "nursery door," child-height, painted with WRENS; the house made it for its guest. The arch is the front garden route, which loops on itself (dream-logic).
- THE LIE shown: the door is only paint on stone; through the arch, a sunlit garden, and far across it a boy in a drover's smock sitting on the rim of a dry fountain, waving.
- EXTREME consequence (pending Token): the glamour doesn't let go - Hindrance 1 "House-glamour in her eyes" (the lie stays overlaid on her ordinary sight; needs a story fix - salt water, silver, running water, or Kade's hand over her eyes plus a Ward). If Lee Tokens (-> common failure): the lie still shows (the house won), but no lingering Hindrance.
- Lee did NOT spend the Token: Hindrance 1 "House-glamour in her eyes" STANDS (Tokens remain 1/2). Caria believes the lie (the painted door is paint; Jem is in the sunlit garden). She drops the Sight - the garden overlay persists anyway.
- She creeps toward the arch. Kade sees only green shadow and brambles through it. HOLLIS sits down at the arch threshold and will not cross; he looks back at the painted door and whines once. Kade: "Sister. What are you looking at?"
- Caria: "It opens onto a faerie garden... lovely." Sees Hollis; confidence drops; steps back.
- Kade tells her what HE sees: a ruined forecourt - brambles, a collapsed colonnade, dead leaves, a dry basin choked with ivy. No sunlight, no roses, no boy. "The house showed you a door that wasn't and a garden that isn't. Or the other way round."
- As she steps back, the garden-boy stands up on the fountain rim and calls, thin and sweet: "Miss? Don't go." (The house's lie pushing. Not the Caller - it's dead.)
- Kade offers the fix from his trade: salt water to the eyes (he has a canteen; she has Berra's coarse salt). Or silver.
- Story fix: hairpin (silver) clenched in her teeth, salt water from Kade's canteen + Berra's salt rubbed into her eyes. HINDRANCE "House-glamour in her eyes" REMOVED (no roll - a Hindrance takes a story solution, and this is one). Eyes sting and stream for a minute.
- Clear eyes: the ruined forecourt as Kade described; the boy and the voice are gone mid-word. Hollis gets up and trots to the painted door, sits beneath it, looks up. With clear eyes she notices the door's paint for the first time: small brown birds on the panels - WRENS - with folded wings, like the one Jem carved in Berra's loft.
- Caria catches herself before addressing the house directly (good instinct). Thanks Hollis; tells Kade about Jem's carved wren. Touches a painted wren: the paint is WARM, like a hearthstone; faintly, from behind solid stone, a few notes hummed - "Little brown wren, where have you been" (the tune Dunny named). GM HIDDEN (@@*): the nursery door opens for someone who answers the song (sings the next line / the lullaby) - a guest-key. Caria only knows the first line (from Dunny). Performer is her tool; Kade knows none of it.
- Kade on the wrens: grim nod - "It built him a door." Asks what she means by "again."
- Caria sings "Little brown wren, where have you been" to the door in the dream-notes. ROLL Performer EL3 -> 0 1 0 = 1 vs house Activation 1 -> 1. TIE -> door stays shut (pending Token, 1/2).
- If it stands: the house hums back the NEXT line's first notes and stops - the song is call-and-response; it wants the ANSWER line. Door doesn't open. Activation stays 1 (singing isn't Sight). OPEN to Lee: Caria grew up in a potato-patch cottage with a hedge-witch grandmother - does she know the rest of the lullaby? (A common country song - Lee's call.)
- Caria asks Kade for the verse; he knows only a barracks version "not fit for a door, let alone a Sister." ROLL recall, base Lv2 vs Diff 1 -> 1 1 = 2 -> effect 1: she remembers her grandmother singing it on the step shelling peas. Answer line (@@* invented): "Down in the hedgerow, where no one has seen." She has the call and answer of verse one only; the later verses are fog.
- Caria sings the answer line. No roll - it's the key. The nursery door OPENS for the answer.
- GM HIDDEN (@@*): by answering as the child, she has been received as a GUEST of the house (guest-right, Accords): the house may not harm a guest under its roof - and a guest may not take from the house. Jem is the house's guest too. Taking him out may count as TAKING, unless it's done as a proper leave-taking (or he walks out of his own will). Kade and Hollis entered with her, so they're guests of her company.
- Beyond the door: a narrow child-height stair spiralling DOWN into the wall's thickness, walls painted with wrens, warm air smelling of hay, milk, and woodsmoke - a farmhouse loft smell. Faint lamplight below. ~quarter to 3 pm outside (fey-time inside may differ).
- Lee canon: guest-right = an Agreement; parties can't DIRECTLY harm each other (a willing guest can be "dined on," tit for tat). Caria requests Kade teach her the barracks version before they part (Kade: "If we live, Sister, I'll teach you every verse, and you'll wish I hadn't.")
- Scene 5: the wren stair and the nursery-sidhe. New scene -> Caria Tokens 1 -> 2/2. GM 3/3.
- Down the stair (about three turns - longer than the wall could hold): a low warm room made as a farmhouse loft - hay, rafters, a lamp on a hook, a patched quilt. JEM asleep in the hay: thin, pale, smiling, breathing slow, hair grown long (fey-time ~ a night for him; months outside). Beside him in a rocking chair: a WOMAN-SHAPE made of lamplight and dust and dry leaves - the HOUSE's own manifestation playing his mother, humming the wren song and rocking. Hollis stands rigid at the foot of the stair. The mother-shape stops humming and turns its head toward them.
- Lee: the stair turned WIDDERSHINS. GM confirms: three turns against the sun going down. (@@* fey logic: the way OUT must be walked sunwise - deosil - or it leads back down.)
- Caria steps in, touches Hollis (understood), waits for Kade. Kade folds through and stands silent - he knows the rules.
- The house's mother-shape rises, puts a leaf-finger to where lips would be: "Hush. He's sleeping." (echoes Aubric's "Hush" - the house borrows a mother's manner, and Jem's mother's voice, Tessa (@@*)). Then, as a hostess: "You've come a long way. Sit. There's milk warm, and bread." A jug and a loaf appear on a stool that wasn't there. FEY FOOD - accepting it under guest-right = an Agreement term (tit for tat); eating fey food classically binds you to the place.
- Hunter's Blind carried into Scene 5 (duration: until there's a reason it shouldn't). Hook available again (new scene).
- THIRD EYE peek at the nursery: EL4 -> 0 1 1 0 = 2 vs house (Activation 1 + own heart 1 = 2 dice) -> 1 0 = 1. EFFECT 1, truth seen. Her 2 successes are absorbed by the Blind -> Activation stays 1.
- SEEN: the loft is dream-skin over a damp root-vault; hay = dead leaves and moss; lamp = foxfire fungus. Jem is REAL, on moss, thin, cold, breathing. The Caller's old line from him hangs slack and grey, dissolving (it's dead). The dream is WOVEN FROM THE LEY CURRENT - the drain she felt comes in here; the house spends the Crossroads current to keep Jem's dream going. (That's why Crossroads' current runs thin.) The woman-shape is the HOUSE itself - vast, old, lonely, not malicious: an old hound keeping a pup. The "milk" is liquid current; the "bread" is moss. Eating/drinking = taking the house's current into yourself = joining the dream.
- Caria feels empathy: it wants someone to care for, someone to call it home. Asks (in herself) whether such an entity can keep a non-fae charge without harming it.
- Her own read (no roll; she can see Jem): as it stands, NO. It feeds dream and current, not bread; the mind is kept, the body withers. Mortals can't live on what it gives. Things that CAN live on current/dream: spirits, fey, a spark of fire, a place's own small gods. (GM @@*: possible solutions she may find - give it a charge that can live on current (a spirit/sprite/ember), a mortal STEWARD who comes and goes (a shrine-keeper arrangement, like Rook's), or an Agreement returning the Crossroads current. Don't hand it to her.)
- The hostess notices her tears, steps closer: "Oh, love. Don't cry. You're home now."
- Caria to the house: "No. But I am here, and I see you. I must think for a moment. I will decide what I need; it shouldn't take long." (A clear refusal of "you're home now" - player's choice, no roll forced.) The house honors guest-right: it steps back to the rocking chair, sits, and waits, humming very softly. Being SEEN has moved it - nobody has looked at it as itself in centuries. (@@* GM: the house's disposition toward Caria improves - a Boost to any honest negotiation with it.)
- LEE CANON (Caria backstory): her mother was ill and wanted nothing to do with her; her father said it was her fault and she should have died in the womb, and later SOLD her to the Monastery. Gram (hedge-witch grandmother) was the only one who made her feel wanted, but never once let her inside her dirt-floor hovel in the woods - they gardened and played in the rain, and Gram sent her home wet, with a kiss but no invitation in. At the Monastery, only Ivro and Mags ever showed her more than cordial attention.
- Kade covers her hand on his shoulder with his own; says only, low: "Take your time. It's waiting. So will I." Hollis leans against her legs.
- Caria recovers; scratches Hollis's ruff; kneels by Jem without touching (the house's "hush" is being honored implicitly - no accepted obligation). SECOND SIGHT on Jem: EL4 -> 0 0 0 0 = EXTREME FAILURE (third extreme fail on 4 dice this session). House 2d -> 0 1 = 1. No Hook allowed after an extreme fail (Lee's proposed rule). Pending Token (2/2).
- If it stands (GM): her Sight falls INTO Jem's dream. Her body stays kneeling, eyes glazed; her mind is in a spring night in a south-country farmhouse, Jem's mother humming at the hearth. Loss of agency = effectively Down in the waking world (charmed) until she wins free (a resistance roll vs the dream, or Kade's salt/silver/a hand on her, from outside). It's also an opening: Jem is there, awake in his dream, and can be TALKED to.
- If Tokened (-> common failure): she sees only the dream-skin; nothing learned; no trap.
- Lee spends a TOKEN on the extreme-failed Sight -> common failure. Caria Tokens 1/2. RP: she nearly fell in (Gram's voice replacing Jem's mother's; the urge to crawl into the hay), then "...a fire...in the hearth..." - she hauls herself back. (Karma note: strong candidate for Good Fun Karma at session end.)
- Plain-eyed look with a Healer's eye: EL3 (Lv2+Healer1) -> 0 0 1 = 1 vs Diff 1 -> TIE, fails. She gets only the obvious: very thin, pale, lips dry and cracked, hair past his collar, nails long, breath slow and shallow, skin cool. She can't judge how close to the edge he is, or what waking him will do to him.
- Caria to the house: "He is mortal." (No roll - a statement of truth.)
- The house's answer (GM, @@*): it stops rocking. "I know. They all are." It has kept mortal sleepers before - the Caller's leavings, strays who wandered in - and they always FADE, "like leaves, in the end, no matter how I keep them." It believes fading is simply what mortals do. It does NOT understand that its keeping (dream and current, no bread or water or sun) is what kills them. It kept the last one "through three winters." It is grieving in advance for Jem.
- Caria: "It is. ... Do you love him?" House: "Yes." Then, groping for mortal words: "As a hearth loves whoever sits beside it. As a roof loves what sleeps beneath it. He came down my stair singing for his mother, and I had no one, and he had no one here, and so." Its love is real - and possessive, the way a house holds what is inside it.
- Caria's plea and offer (she sits before the chair as a confidante): let me speak to him; he is dying for love of home; I understand and am grateful; he cannot give you what you need; I might find you a better tenant, able to love you and to live on what you give.
- ROLL Mediation: EL5 (Lv2+Perf1+Med1 +1 "seen" Boost) -> 1 1 0 1 1 = 4 vs house hold EL3 -> 0 1 1 = 2 -> EFFECT 2. SUCCESS.
- House's answer (GM @@*): it cannot WAKE him - waking is not in it. The wren song has a last verse, the one where the wren flies home; singing it ends the dream (a mother's release). The house will sing it IF Caria gives her word: an AGREEMENT under the Accords - Caria will bring the house a tenant who can stay (one that can live on current and dream: a spirit, a fey-kin, a spark). Terms proposed by the house: her word, no deadline named YET ("before the leaves turn twice" if pressed). In return: it sings the last verse, Jem wakes, and it lets him go "if he will go." And it asks that someone sing to it once more before they leave.
- AGREEMENT MADE (Accords): Caria answers by singing - taken as her WORD: she will bring the house a tenant who can stay (one that can live on current and dream). No deadline named. In return the house sings the last verse of the wren song to release Jem, "if he will go." Her song fulfils the house's second request (to be sung to).
- CARIA'S PRIVATE RESOLVE (Lee): if she cannot find a suitable tenant, she will BE one - at worst her spirit drawn here to sleep forever. (GM @@*: the house doesn't know this. Note - a demon-touched soul, Hint of Brimstone, might in fact suit it. Latent.)
- She sings "Hush, Little Baby" (Gram's version - "Gramma's gonna buy you a mockingbird"), weeping, remembering Gram's squash-doll with twig legs. (Strong Good Fun Karma candidate.)
- The house, sung to for the first time since "the Lady" (Sarina) went away: its leaves go still; the fungus-light warms toward true gold. Then it begins the LAST VERSE of the wren song (@@* invented lyric): "Little brown wren, the long night is done / Fly home, little wren, to the door and the sun." Jem stirs.
- Caria wakes Jem gently: hair smoothed, cold hand chafed (his hands are free - the "quilt" is lichen), Ivro's kerchief (still faintly smelling of Ivro) used to wipe his face and shade his eyes, cheek to his, voice in his ear.
- ROLL: EL4 (Lv2+Healer1 +1 care Boost) -> 1 1 1 0 = 3 vs shock/dream-pull 2 -> 1 1 = 2 -> EFFECT 1. He wakes WITHOUT shock - but weak, cold, and badly disoriented. First word, cracked from disuse: "Ma?" He believes it's still the night after the fork, last spring. He can barely lift his hand. The choice to go is still his (house's term).
- Caria soothes Jem in Green Haven patois: "Mes Korrin'll not be botherin' ye... ye've been ill... Yer Ma will be glad to have ye back." (GM note to Lee OOC: Korrin told Caria in S2 that Jem's mother died two winters ago.) Kade hands over the canteen (fresh water).
- Jem sips (Healer: small sips; he coughs, then drinks - the first true water in months; it tastes like iron and cold after the dream's milk). As the water lands, memory surfaces: "Ma's... Ma's dead. She's been dead since the winter before last." The dream falling away hits him as fresh grief. He starts to cry, weakly, and asks where he is.
- (Lee: "Yer Ma" was a slip, but still true for a Conjuror.) Caria: even gone, his Ma loves him and is glad he's safe; asks him to trust her a minute; more water; hands the canteen to Kade.
- FIAT LUX (Fax Iacta, plain cast): EL4 -> 1 1 1 0 = 3 vs Diff 2 (placed near without harm or igniting leaves) -> effect 1. A steady blue-white flame the size of a fist hangs a hand's breadth above the moss at Jem's side, warm on his face and hands; nothing catches.
- THE HOUSE reacts: a REAL fire in its heart for the first time in centuries. Its leaves shiver - then the whole leaf-shape LEANS toward the flame the way a cat leans to a hearth. It whispers: "...a hearth." (Seed of the tenant idea: a fire that can live on current. GM does NOT spell it out.)
- Jem warms, calms a little under the kerchief; Kade gives him sips.
- Caria looks around the vault by her flame's light (no roll): at the far end, behind a curtain of roots, a great HEARTH of fitted dark stone, man-high, carved with salamanders and flames - cold for centuries, choked with dead leaves and root-tangle. (GM @@*: the house once had a hearth-spirit tenant - "the Lady's fire" - which went out when Sarina was erased. That's the vacancy. The house has been cold since.)
- Caria calls Asher: EL6 (Lv2+Conj2+True Name 2) vs Diff 3 (miles + sidhe wall) -> 1 0 1 1 1 0 = 4 -> effect 1. She reaches him, faint, like a voice down a well. Asher is at Tanner's Ford with the caravan, just making camp (~5 pm outside - fey-time made the afternoon run fast). All safe there; he's building the bonfire. He's relieved and a little frantic.
- Caria to the house: "Will you accept a gift, and allow a guest for me?" - strides to the great hearth.
- House (GM): "Yes." Then, remembering its manners, formally: "Your guest is my guest, under this roof." (Accords: a gift accepted puts the house in HER debt - this may square or partly square the tenant Agreement, depending on the gift.) It rises from the chair and follows her to the hearth, leaves whispering, and stands looking at the cold firebox the way you'd look at an empty cradle.
- Still pending: WHAT is the gift/guest? Carried: Old Bellows's live coal (promised to the SEA), applewood + forge charcoal, oil, amber, Fax Iacta, Asher (miles off at the Ford).
- Caria declines to use Bellows's coal ("not your task, my friend; I will honor my promise to you yet").
- "Fiat conflagratio!" - Fax Iacta, Improvised Persistent, into the great hearth. EL5 (Lv2+Role1+Fax1+Improv1) -> 1 0 0 1 0 = 2 vs Diff 2 (man-high hearth, fuelless, fey house) -> TIE, the hold FAILS, pending Lee: Hook (+2 successes -> 4 vs 2, effect 2; brimstone in the house's new hearth-fire), last Token (-> 5 vs 2, effect 3), or Karma "Succeed". If it stands: Fax Iacta destabilizes (lost until rebound; Fiat Lux by Jem goes out).
- Lee pre-announced: once the spell holds, spend 1 hard Karma to make Persistent PERMANENT. Book support: spells.md - "Enchantment creates 'permanent' magical effects with extensive rituals and Karma expenditure, with similar costs to Role and Maneuver advancement." beyond.md - any Maneuver point permanently spent on an entity creates a PERMANENT ANCHOR LINE to it (traceable back to its creator).
- Lee spends the LAST TOKEN -> 5 vs 2, EFFECT 3. Caria Tokens 0/2. The hearth-fire HOLDS: blue-white root, gold heart, filling the great firebox, burning on the house's own current (no fuel); leaf-rot and roots don't catch.
- ENCHANTMENT: Caria spends 1 hard KARMA to make the Persistent permanent (spells.md Enchantment; cost like a new Maneuver). Karma 5 -> 4 in hand (+1 earmarked Conjuror). PERMANENT ANCHOR LINE: the hearth-fire of the Weeping Court is tied to Caria for good (beyond.md) - she can always find it; anything that knows how can follow the line back to her. A piece of her is now in that fire. Fax Iacta binding is unaffected.
- @@ UNCLEAR: does an in-play Enchantment Karma spend count as her one improvement this session? (spending-karma.md: one score per session, at the end.) Ask Lee at wrap.
- HOUSE: accepts the gift. It feels the piece of her in the fire - "There is something of you in it." Salamanders on the mantel seem to move in the firelight. GM (@@*): a hearth-fire burning long enough on a fey house's current may one day wake a salamander - a true tenant. The house declares the Agreement KEPT: "You have given me more than a tenant. You have given me a hearth, and a little of yourself to keep it." (Payment accepted concludes the Agreement - Accords ruling.) The anchor remains.
- Latent: with Jem's dream ended and the fire fed modestly, the drain on the Crossroads current will lessen - it will run fuller within days.
- Caria to the house: "And yet I still have a guest to whom I wish to introduce you, if I am able." Speaks to Asher THROUGH the hearth-fire. ROLL EL6 -> 0 1 0 1 0 0 = 2 vs Diff 1 -> effect 1. Clear now - fire to fire, like talking across a table. Asher can come by way of fire, bonfire to hearth. He hesitates: "You told me to keep the camp fire. If I come, it's just a fire." (GM hidden: the ghola is still loose between the ruin and the coast; the Caller is dead. Asher doesn't know about the ghola.) Awaiting Caria's word.
- Caria to Asher: life is risk; you gave them a great fire and saw they're not being silly; can't you go back from here? Asher: yes, it's close fire to fire. He COMES THROUGH: the hearth flares; a gold spark bursts out, circles the vault, and lands on the mantel among the carved salamanders. Under the Accords he enters as HER guest (guest-right extends). Asher introduces himself to the house with proper spirit courtesy - use-name only, "Asher, of Caria's hearth" (no true name). Awed by "her" fire; notices the piece of her in it.
- Camp at the Ford: the bonfire is now just a fire (Asher absent). GM hidden: the ghola is still out there; watch for consequences if Asher stays away long.
- Caria offers: Asher as a guest the house can give treats to, who won't fade; he may come again after they're gone - news of the world, fireside company, bring her love from afar.
- Asher (his own consent - "Asher belongs to Asher"): YES, eagerly - a hearth that tastes of Caria, a lady who speaks courtesy, and treats. His terms: he comes when he chooses, and he stays hers. Accords: hospitality given and company given - "Both. So neither." No debt. (GM @@*: Asher visiting a fey house's current-fed hearth will grow him over time - a latent power bump.)
- The house accepts with joy. It offers him a "treat" at once: a curl of the current, like a sip of honey-light, from the fire. Asher (to Caria, privately): "Oh. OH. Caria. Can I keep her?"
- Caria to Asher: Asher is Asher's, the House is the House's; make whatever bargains please you both.
- To Jem: nearly dead but no - alive, fey-touched, missed; Korrin grieves; let's get you back whole. Wraps him in her yellow robe and sash (she's in her under-layers now: brass breastplate, bracers, skirt with Salt in the Hem). Fiat Lux went out when she cast the hearth-fire (Lee). Warms Jem by the great hearth a few minutes. He can stand only leaning, legs shaking; can't walk more than a few steps.
- Kade: he'll carry the boy - "He weighs less than my pack." No to drovers in the woods at night: "There's more than one thing loose on this coast tonight. My business, not yours." (ghola hint). Suggests sending Asher by fire to Korrin: a wagon and two lads with lanterns to the ROAD at the woods-stretch - on the road, not into the trees.
- The House: "My woods will not keep you, guests of mine." It will light their way - the boundary stones will glow along the deer-track. Warning: "Go up my stair SUNWISE, or it will bring you back down to me." To Jem it offers a farewell - it asks him, formally, "Will you go, little wren?" (his choice - the Agreement's term).
- Caria to Jem: he may go back to the dream if he chooses with a clear mind - no judgment; she envies the time he had; it will haunt him but needn't hurt; but he WILL die, and Korrin will hold it against them both; he need not fear the House.
- ROLL (Performer/Mediation, honest free choice): EL5 (Lv2+Perf1+Med1 +1 Boost: she offered the dream back honestly, no manipulation) -> 1 1 1 1 1 = 5 EXTREME SUCCESS vs grief-pull EL2 -> 1 0 = 1. EFFECT 4.
  - Extreme success: +1 free Token (Caria 0 -> 1/2); +1 Karma earmarked PERFORMER.
  - Outcome (GM): Jem chooses clear-headed, in his own words. The haunting will be a sweet one (no lingering pull back to the Court; no Complication). He thanks the house with a child's courtesy - which under the Accords is a gift of thanks given freely; the house answers with a farewell gift of its own (a single dry oak leaf that stays warm - @@* minor keepsake, no mechanical weight unless Lee wants one). Latent: the boy now knows the house as a friend, not a monster.
- Caria to Kade: "I'll be right behind you." (Asher's errand NOT yet sent.)
- Caria to Asher: if the House counts him welcome he should come often, for the good of both; she hopes he still hears her call at need; this fire is a place for his own rest, "no place closer to my heart."
- Caria to the House: thanks as a host(ess); asks if she may return; says the House may call Asher here, and may call HER at need - she will attend if able, "barring only some very pressing duty." "Are we in Accord?"
- GM (no roll - a mutual offer freely accepted, goodwill high after the extreme success): SECOND AGREEMENT under the Accords, balanced (promise for promise, no debt):
  - Caria: standing welcome as guest of the Weeping Court (guest-right renewable on each visit); she will answer the House's call at need, barring pressing duty.
  - The House: CARIA judges what counts as pressing ("I trust the hand that lit my hearth"). It promises to try not to call her for small things - "I do not yet know which things are small." (Latent: its first calls may be for things a mortal would think trivial.)
  - Gender: the House says the Lady (Sarina) called it "she," and it liked being called something by someone. Hostess is fine. (@@* flavor.)
  - Channels: the House can call through the hearth-fire (Asher hears it there) and, GM hidden, along Caria's permanent anchor line - she'll feel it as warmth/woodsmoke and a pull toward the Court.
- Asher: accepts the standing invitation; "I'll hear you. Fire or no fire, I'll hear you." (GM: this is his sincere belief, not a new mechanic - distance calls still roll.) Then reminds her: the Ford bonfire is untended by him and she hasn't said about the wagon. Awaiting Caria's word.
- Caria to Asher: Korrin will be at camp, wagons circled, silver in teeth, nail in fist (smirk); ask her to send a wagon to the road-point nearest the ruin. Asher goes by fire (hearth -> Ford bonfire, no roll: fire-to-fire, he's made the trip once already). Ford bonfire tended again.
- Exit: Caria climbs the stair SUNWISE, catches up with Kade (carrying Jem) and Hollis at the top. Out the painted door (it opens from inside freely for guests leaving).
- GM ORACLE (@@ MISSING - L1 has no random-encounter/oracle procedure; used a 2d pool: 2=contact, 1=sign, 0=nothing): ghola near the woods road tonight? -> 0 0 = 0. NOTHING. Walk out is quiet.
- Boundary stones glow along the deer-track (House keeps its word). Night; cold after the warm vault; Caria in under-layers only (robe on Jem). Road reached ~2 hrs after dusk. Wagon lanterns approach from the Ford direction: Korrin is driving it herself, two drovers with lanterns.

## END OF S03 (Lee called the wrap, 2026-09-29 ~16:26) - on the road, Korrin's wagon just stopped (reunion unplayed)
