## SESSION 4 - started 2026-09-29 17:35 (Lee's clock)
- Caria now Level 3: Luck Tokens 3/3. +1 attendance Karma -> 3 carried (+1 earmarked Conjuror); cap 4. Hook A Hint of Brimstone 3 (Karma up to 3 triggers/session). GM Tokens 3/3. Harm none (tired - up since before dawn; no mechanical fatigue yet).
- Scene 1: the woods road, ~2 hrs after dusk. Korrin on the wagon box, two drovers with lanterns (@@* Tam and old Wick).
- Caria watches Korrin's eyes as she works out what Kade carries. No roll (plain emotion, lantern-lit, close). Sequence: dread (the way men carry a body), recognition of the yellow robe (Caria's), then Jem's face as the lantern swings - breath, a twitch. Korrin's guilt (hidden: she sent him on the watch that night) shows as a flinch before joy. The nail drops from her teeth.
- Caria walks forward (limping: bramble run, cold stone, deep aches from near-misses with power - COLOR per Lee, no Harm assigned) and answers with her face: pride, joy, relief. No words.
- Korrin reads it. Nearly falls off the box getting down. Goes to Kade, touches Jem's face. Jem half-wakes: "Mes Korrin... you'll skin me." Korrin: a sound between a laugh and a sob - "After I feed you." Tam (lantern) is crying openly.
- Korrin sees Caria shivering in brass; strips off her own oilskin drover's coat and puts it on her without asking (a gift freely given - Accords: Korrin's hospitality, no debt claimed).
- Korrin, low, to Caria only: "Whatever your mountain charges for this, Sister, I'll pay it." (Order etiquette: a Rikitak priest never names a price for aid unasked; Korrin doesn't know the rule. Accords: an OFFER of payment, not yet accepted. Caria's answer decides whether a debt exists.)
- Caria declines payment: "No mountain. Just me, an honest pilgrim, and Kade, and Hollis... and the tenets I was taught, whatever the men in that building say." ACCORDS: offer declined -> no debt either way. Order etiquette upheld (no price named). Korrin (who hates the road tax) hears a Sister of the mountain disown the mountain's prices - lands hard, in Caria's favor. (@@* latent: Korrin will say so to others; the story travels, and it is a kinder one than Fennick's.)
- Kade speaks for himself (@@*): "The Almoner paid my passage. I carried a boy a mile. A bowl for the dog, and nobody writes my name in anything." Korrin: "Done." (Kade keeps his hands clean and his name out of Calder's books - consistent.)
- Into the wagon: Jem bedded on sacking and blankets between Caria and Korrin; Kade and Hollis walk beside with Tam's lantern. Turned for Tanner's Ford, ~an hour's roll.
- Korrin asks Caria, quietly: "Where was he, Sister?"
- Caria curls close to Jem in the wagon (warmth; propriety bedamned). To Korrin: "Dreaming of his home, and his mother. Oh, such sweet dreams, I envy them." Shows her the leaf. Pats Korrin's hand; falls asleep.
- Korrin (GM): country-bred; she knows a fey gift when she sees one and won't touch it. "Don't let him lose that." Asks nothing more.
- GM ORACLES (2d, same procedure as S3): (a) ghola troubles the Ford camp tonight -> 1 1 = 2 CONTACT. (b) Caria dreams tonight -> 1 1 = 2 A REAL DREAM-VISIT.
- GM HIDDEN (@@*), decided now:
  - The dream-visitor is TESSA, Jem's mother - her genuine shade, not the House's copy and not a lure (the Caller is dead). She has lingered two winters because her boy was "neither living nor dead" in the sidhe; with him home she can go on. She came to thank the one who brought him back - and to WARN: she has seen something at the edge of the Ford's firelight that "was a man once": hungry, sad, drawn by the smell of a weak, fey-touched boy. (Jem is the likeliest target: weak, fey-marked, easy meat.)
  - Mirror for Caria: the dream opens on a doorstep - a woman shelling peas - and unlike Gram, this woman invites her IN. Accords question for Caria: accepting hospitality from the dead in a dream. (GM truth: Tessa means only kindness; a dead woman's welcome costs nothing but may leave a thread - @@* a small tie to the Dreamlands, not harmful.)
  - The ghola: Calder's lost "property." Contact tonight = it comes to the edge of the Ford camp in the small hours, after the wagon arrives. Defenses in play: silver in every drover's teeth, cold-iron kegs on wagon 2, Asher's bonfire, Kade + Hollis (Kade HUNTS it). Resolve when it arrives.
- Lee (OOC): how lucid is Caria in the dream? No rule in the book (@@ MISSING). GM ruling (@@*): a visited dreamer rolls Conjuror (or Level if untrained) vs the dream's hold; success = lucid. ROLL: EL5 (Lv3+Conj2) -> 0 0 1 1 1 = 3 vs hold EL2 -> 1 0 = 1. EFFECT 2: LUCID.
  - She knows she's dreaming; knows this is a VISIT (someone else's dream-place, not her own); knows her body is asleep in Korrin's wagon beside Jem; sees the woman as a shade (dead) and sees Jem in her face. Her Conjuror senses work here at normal EL (Sight, Third Eye, Rebuke, Wards). What she does NOT get from lucidity alone: whether the visitor means well. That takes a look.
- Lee: lucidity ruling ADOPTED. Caria thanks the woman and goes in as a GUEST, treating it as a visit to a real home - both knowing she's dead, the Accords binding. ACCORDS: guest-right Agreement with Tessa's shade (no direct harm either way).
- Inside (GM): a one-room cottage, hearth, table, a cradle-that-was, a boy's too-small boots by the door. Tessa sets bread and a cup of milk before her but doesn't press ("if you'd like"). Tessa: she stayed because Jem was "neither here nor gone"; she watched the road, sang to him when she could reach; now he's home she can go on - soon, not tonight. She thanks Caria. Then the WARNING (see oracle): a thing at the edge of the Ford's firelight "that was a man once - hungry, and sad, and it's smelled my boy."
- Bread/milk from the dead: Caria's choice pending. (GM truth @@*: hospitality offered freely by the host is part of guest-right - no debt; but food eaten in a dream-place leaves a small thread to it. Not harmful.)
- Caria: "It will not have him." (heat surprises her). "Fey? Demon? How best to fight it?"
- GM HIDDEN (@@*), the ghola decided now, grounded in conjuring.md ("Halfling Dead might be Zombies, Ghouls, or Revenants"): a HALFLING DEAD - a dead man's shade held in its own corpse by a working, not by its own will. The working is a LEASH (Calder's binding; line runs NORTH toward the Monastery). Driven by hunger (the binding feeds on what it eats) and by grief (it wants to go home, like Tessa did). Iron: nothing to it (not fey). Silver: bites it (Death - Lee canon). Fire/light: it avoids being SEEN more than it fears burning. It can be killed (destroy the body) or LAID (cut the leash + resolve its grief -> the shade goes on, the body falls). Kade's job is to RETURN it to Calder - conflict of aims latent.
- Tessa's answer (what a farm-wife shade can see, no roll - she's a witness): "Not the Fair Folk. Not the other kind either. It's DEAD, love. Like me. Only it's still in its meat, and it shouldn't be." It stayed just past the light; circled the drovers "with the coins in their mouths like they were nettles"; stopped dead when the fire flared gold (Asher). A STRING on it "like a dog's lead," running back north up the road. It wants to go home too, she thinks. "I'm a farm wife, not a priest. But if it were me, I'd want someone to cut that string."
- It came to the edge of the camp near moonset; it will come again, closer, when the fire burns low. Jem is the weakest, most fey-marked thing in the camp.
- LEE CANON: a ghola carries a TRACE OF DEMON as well - "they're nasty." GM correction in-fiction: Tessa (a farm-wife shade, no expert) qualifies her "not the other kind": under the grave-smell there WAS a sulphur note, "like yours, love, but spoilt." Update ghola profile: Halfling Dead + demonic taint (silver bites both; consecrated things likely too).
- Caria (prayer): thank the gods - it's the DEAD (her Order's ground). Then catches the word: "Walked. Like a dog."
- GM HIDDEN (@@*), follows from what Tessa already said (the lead pulls it back when it turns aside): it is not merely lost - someone at the far end is STEERING it. Calder, from the north, walking it south along the coast road on a long lead; it strays to feed and to try for home, and gets yanked back on course. Destination @@ undecided (Saltwind is the obvious candidate - where Calder was humiliated). Kade was sent to "find" it - whether he knows it's being steered is @@ open (lean: he doesn't; he'd be furious).
- Tessa's answer: "Walked. Aye. Like a dog on a lead - and whoever's at the other end knows where they want it. South. Down your road." She can't say who.
- Caria: "Silver for the demon. Salt for the dead." (Lee's working folk-canon; consistent with S3 silver canon.) Thanks Tessa; offers payment in kind - something to carry to Jem for comfort.
- ACCORDS: the warning was a favor (debt on Caria); Tessa ACCEPTS payment in kind -> Agreement: Caria carries her message to Jem. When delivered, concluded.
- Tessa's message (@@* GM): "Tell him I'm not cross about the barley. He'll know. Tell him to keep his feet dry - and when he has little ones of his own, to sing them all the verses." (The barley: private between them - proof it's really her. Jem will know.) She kisses Caria's brow - cool as a draft - and lets her go.
- Caria WAKES in the wagon at Tanner's Ford, ~2.5 hrs after dusk. Bonfire high and gold (Asher). Korrin's hushed "don't wake the Sister." Jem asleep and warm. Kade and Hollis by the fire. The ghola not yet returned (it comes when the fire burns low - small hours).
- Caria wakes gasping; "Salt! SALT!" Climbs out (minding Jem's blanket), dumps the tied bag of Korrin's nails at Korrin's feet (property returned - account closed), demands a salt circle round the wagon NOW and round the whole camp if there's enough. (Aside: she wonders why salt fish travel SOUTH to the coast - waved off.)
- GM: the salt fish (@@*) is upland lake-pike and eel from the Mere country, dry-salted, bound for Saltwind's inland buyers and the ships' stores - sea-towns don't salt freshwater fish. Four casks, packed in coarse grey salt between layers; plus the cook's salt sack. Enough for the wagon easily; for the whole camp ring (11 wagons circled at the Ford, ~70 paces round) - enough for a THIN unbroken line, if laid carefully. (Korrin will not love emptying her fish casks; she does it anyway.)
- Korrin: no questions, instant action (tonight's credit). Orders Wick and Tam to crack the fish casks.
- Kade: rises at "salt" and "circle" - he knows what salt rings are for. What he was cleaning: a coil of fine SILVER CHAIN with a small lock (@@* his tool for taking the ghola back - a silver leash). Caria sees it. He asks where she heard of the dead.
- MECHANICS PENDING: a salt circle is inert until invoked (Salt-in-the-Hem canon: "the Lore of salt protects, not the salt"). Laying it = labor; binding it = a Conjuror Ward, keyed to the Dead (wager capped at Conjuror 2). Camp-sized ring = higher Difficulty than the wagon ring. Ghola arrives when the fire burns low (small hours).
- Caria to Asher (mindspeech): the dead are afoot with a demon taint; patrol the edge of the light, smell first, spot and mark better, be careful.
- Caria to Kade: "You're up, Finder. I suspect you won't have far to look tonight." (She does NOT answer "who told you" - Kade notes the dodge, lets it lie for now.) Kade (@@*): takes Hollis toward the reeds; says quietly first: "If it comes to the salt, Sister - I need it whole. The Hierarch wants his property back." (The conflict out loud: Kade's job is RETURN, not release.)
- Salt ring: laid by Wick, Tam and Caria round the whole circle of wagons, thin but unbroken; Caria walks it by lantern and has Tam check behind every other pourer. Wagon ring (Jem's) laid thick. Both INERT until Caria invokes them (Ward roll pending). She finally drinks (water, then a swallow of her brandy).
- ROLL Asher patrol: EL3 (Lv2 +1 knows both reeks) -> 0 0 0 = EXTREME FAILURE vs ghola hide EL3 -> 0 1 1 = 2. Pending Lee: Token on her spirit's roll? (@@ UNCLEAR - may a player spend a Luck Token on her summoned spirit's roll? GM leaning yes - it's her working, her Maneuver - True Name.) Token vs extreme -> common failure (Lee ruling).
  - If it stands: Asher comes back with a confident ALL-CLEAR ("frogs and river-stink, nothing dead"). FALSE. The ghola let him pass within a few feet in the reeds, and has now taken his measure: small, bright, and he LEAVES THE FIRE when he patrols. It will move when he's away. While Asher was out, the bonfire was plain yellow.
  - If Tokened (common failure): Asher finds nothing, and says so honestly - "nothing I could find, which isn't the same as nothing." No false clear; the ghola still hidden.
- LEE RULING: Luck Tokens can be spent on ANY roll - her spirit's, another PC's, even an enemy's; the GM may spend GM Tokens on a player's roll if deserved. Lee LEAVES Asher's extreme failure ("It's interesting!") -> it STANDS: false all-clear; the ghola has Asher's measure (small; leaves the fire to patrol).
- Caria to all: "Salt for the dead, SILVER for the demon. No one puts down their silver." To Kade: "pretty good authority from an interested party"; "The Hierarch can have it back whole or in pieces, raw or roasted. I'll not pay one man's life for what HE wants, but I'll do my best to keep YOUR life as uncomplicated as we can." Kade (@@*): "Then we understand each other, Sister." (He does. He'll try to take it alive; he won't trade a drover for it.)
- OUTER WARD: silver hairpin pricks her thumb; one drop of blood to the salt.
  - ROLL blood Boost: EL5 (Lv3+Conj2) -> 0 1 0 0 0 = 1 vs Diff 1 -> 0. EFFECT 1 -> +1 Boost.
  - ROLL Ward: EL6 (Lv3+Conj2+1 blood) - wager 2 -> 4 dice: 0 0 1 1 = 2 vs Diff 2 (camp-wide ring, thin salt) -> 1 0 = 1. EFFECT 1. HOLDS.
  - Result: Ward "SALT AND BLOOD" (@@* name) - rating 2 (wager), keyed to the DEAD, Persistent, Reactive, Area (the whole ring of eleven wagons, per Lee: Wards get Area free). Reactive: when a dead thing tries to cross, the Ward opposes at rating 2. Lasts until there's a reason it shouldn't (a break in the line, dawn? @@ - GM owns the candle-snuffer).
  - Hook NOT used (available once this scene; note: her blood carries the brimstone mark - @@* a demon-tainted thing may find the ward... interesting. Not invoked, no effect).
  - Inner ring (Korrin's wagon / Jem) still INERT.
- Asher returns: bright, pleased - "Frogs and river-stink. Nothing dead out there, Caria, I'd know." FALSE (extreme failure stands). The bonfire goes gold again. GM hidden: the ghola, in the reeds east of the ford, watched him pass at arm's length.
- Caria to Asher: hopefully it stays that way - but the boy's MOTHER said it caught his scent and is coming. Be ready. How to make the inner ring stronger? -> QUERY THE CANDLE (trick: answer = Boost; favor on the debt ledger).
  - Asher's answer (@@* GM): ash. "Ash from a fire I've lived in is mine. Mix it in the salt, and anything dead that crosses that ring crosses ME - I'll feel it like a cold hand in my flame." Also flags the oak leaf: the House's warmth in it, "another lock on his door." -> +1 Boost to the inner Ward, and the ring doubles as an ALARM to Asher.
  - ACCORDS: the Query's favor SQUARES Asher's small S1 debt (as he pointed out smugly in S3). Asher's ledger with her now: even, apart from her standing "you have my heart."
- INNER WARD: hairpin, thumb, blood into the salt mixed with a double handful of bonfire ash.
  - ROLL blood Boost: EL5 -> 1 0 0 1 0 = 2 vs Diff 1 -> 0. EFFECT 2 -> +2 Boost.
  - ROLL Ward: EL8 (Lv3+Conj2+blood 2+ash 1) - wager 2 -> 6 dice: 0 1 0 0 0 1 = 2 vs Diff 1 (one wagon, thick) -> 0. EFFECT 2. HOLDS.
  - Result: Ward "ASH AND SALT" (@@* name) round Korrin's wagon - rating 2 (wager), keyed to the DEAD, Persistent, Reactive, Area; woven with Asher's ash: any dead thing touching it alerts Asher at once, wherever he is.
  - Two wagered Wards now up at once (each wager 2, cap Conjuror 2 each). @@ UNCLEAR logged.
- Resources: Caria Tokens 3/3; Hook unused this scene; Karma 3 (+1 earmarked Conjuror). GM Tokens 3/3.
- Caria realizes: the hairpin is SILVER, and hers, and she can still hold it (silver bites demon taint; her mark doesn't flinch - yet). Combs out her ruined braid, grinning. The white lock is subtly WIDER than she remembered (Lee canon - Hook 3 showing).
- PRAYER, witnessed by Asher, before all gods: if she can save a life at the cost of being able to hold silver, so be it; let her not fall to darkness, but she will pay such a price as she must. (@@* GM: an offer made before the gods, witnessed by a spirit - not a bargain until something ANSWERS it. Latent. Nothing answers tonight. Asher: "Witnessed." - and he's frightened by it. Good Fun Karma candidate.)
- SECOND SIGHT over the reeds: EL5 (Lv3+Conj2) -> 0 0 0 1 1 = 2 vs ghola hide EL3 -> 1 1 1 = 3 EXTREME SUCCESS (enemy). Caria FAILS, pending Lee: Hook (+3 flat -> 5 vs 3, effect 2; brimstone finds brimstone - it feels her find it; Hook TRIGGER -> +1 Karma, first this session) or Token (-> 5 vs 3, effect 2, clean). Caria Tokens 3/3.
  - If it stands: her Sight shows the camp and the reeds - frog-sparks, the heron, Kade (steady cold lamp), Hollis (smoke with eyes), both Wards (frost ring, ember ring) - and NOTHING dead. Enemy extreme: it saw her Sight sweep past like a lantern, and it TASTED her - the brimstone in her, kin to its own spoilt reek. It now knows which one is the priestess, and it is curious about her as well as hungry for the boy.
- Lee: "Intuition - like calls to like." Spends 2 of the Hook's 3 (SPLITTING the owner's Hook - answers the OPEN symmetry question: yes, the owner may split too). 2 + 2 flat = 4 vs 3 -> EFFECT 1. She FINDS it. Hook remaining this scene: 1.
  - Complication: brimstone finds brimstone both ways - it feels her find it and knows her by the smell. HOOK TRIGGER -> +1 Karma (first this session). Caria Karma 3 -> 4 (+1 earmarked Conjuror). Cap 4.
  - What she sees (effect 1 - location and shape, not identity): ~40 paces east of the ford, beyond the frost ring, crouched in the reeds - a man-shaped absence, grey-dark, the outline wrong at the joints. A smear of spoilt sulphur-yellow at its heart (the demon trace). A LEASH: a thin taut line of dull, greasy gold running away NORTH over the water and the road, out of Sight. Grief on it like wet wool. It is watching Korrin's wagon. When she finds it, it turns its head and looks straight back at her. It does not run. Kade is ~30 paces from it, facing the wrong way.
- Caria steps down from the wagon and walks toward the east edge of camp. Casts FAX IACTA, simple, Improvised GUIDABLE, above her hand: EL6 (Lv3+Spellweaver1+Fax1+Improv1) -> 1 0 1 0 1 1 = 4 vs Diff 2 (1 small flame +1 Guidable) -> 0 1 = 1. EFFECT 3. A fist-sized blue-white flame with a gold heart, steady, answers her will like a hound.
  - @@ UNCLEAR noted: does an Improvised modifier (Guidable) add +1 Difficulty, or is the Improvisation Maneuver's +1 simply the bonus for doing it at all? Ran it as +1 Diff, offset by the Maneuver.
- Ghola: stays. Rises from its crouch at the sight of her light - does not come closer, does not back off. Hollis's hackles go up; he swings round toward it. Kade turns, follows Caria's eyes, sees nothing with plain sight - asks "Where?"
- Caria sends the Fax out over the water to hang an arm's length above and before the ghola: "There." Stops at the Ward line. (No roll - effect 3 Guidable covers 40 paces.)
- LEE NOTE -> GM RULING (OK): a caster can manipulate her own spell and her own Ward so they don't fight; the Ward is keyed to the DEAD, and her flame isn't dead. Passes freely.
- GM HIDDEN (@@* - big canon choice, decided now and written before narrating): the ghola is BROTHER AUBRIC - the late Hospitaller, Caria's old friend, dead of "mushroom tea" (murdered; Calder's faction). Calder raised him as a ghola (Halfling Dead + demon taint), leashed. The grief is Aubric's; "trying for home" east = his old circuit; the head-tilt = he almost knows her voice. Some of Aubric is still in there. (Ties: the Caller used Aubric's voice in S2 because Caria loves him; Kade's job is to return him to Calder; Caria's prayer about silver and saving a life; Lee may veto - it's his friend.)
- Plain-eye reveal in the flame's blue-white light (partial, NOT confirmed to Caria yet): a gaunt man, grey skin tight on the bones, filmed eyes; rags of a robe gone mud-colored (orange reads brown-grey under blue light); a healer's satchel strap still across the chest; a ring of small brass keys on a cord at its belt, like a steward's (the Hospitaller keeps the guest-house keys). It throws up an arm against the light, rasps, does not run (the leash). Drovers see it now - shouts. Hollis bays. Kade starts forward along the bank, chain in hand, ~30 paces from it.
- Caria brings the flame down and a little left to see the KEYS. She is surprisingly calm. No roll (good light, an object she knows).
  - The keys (@@*): six small brass keys and one iron; the iron one (the infirmary poison-cupboard) has a scrap of faded ORANGE ribbon knotted through its bow - Aubric tied it there because his eyes were going and he'd once grabbed the wrong key in the dark. Caria watched him tie it, years ago. CONFIRMATION for Caria: it is Aubric.
  - Ghola: holds still under the light; the head-tilt; its filmed eyes on her. Kade ~20 paces from it now, slowing - he's seen her face change.
- Live sheet delivered to Lee (S04).
- Caria remembers Aubric's dignity, his soul. Slides the silver pin into her lapel (in reach). To Asher: how best to break a spirit's hold on a man's corpse? (A question about spirits to Asher = QUERY THE CANDLE by definition -> +1 Boost; the favor goes on the ledger: Caria now owes Asher a small favor again.)
- ROLL Memory of Aubric as a Boost: EL5 (Lv3+Conj2 - spirit work; Lee may prefer another Role) -> 0 1 0 1 1 = 3 vs Diff 1 -> 1. EFFECT 2 -> "MEMORY OF AUBRIC" +2 Boost (to anything that reaches the man inside: calling, laying, persuading his shade).
- Asher's answer (GM, consistent with the hidden truth):
  1. Don't just burn the meat. Burn it and the shade's loose, but not FREE - the string would drag him north to whoever holds the other end. Worse than now.
  2. Cut the string first. Silver bites it (it's greasy with the same spoilt brimstone). "You did it before, to the fey-thing's hook - down the line." (HINT: a Rebuke down the leash would travel NORTH to its holder. GM HIDDEN: the holder is CALDER. A Rebuke that reaches him = he feels it, knows her flavor; Purification clock consequences; also real harm to him. The leash is a working of rating ~3 (@@*).)
  3. Then he has to WANT to go. "He's sad, Caria. That's the part you're good at."
- Boosts in hand: Memory of Aubric +2, Query +1 (Asher). Hook left this scene: 1. Tokens 3/3.
- Caria SUMMONS Aubric's mind to the fore by his proper name and title ("Hospitaller Aubric de Ganth, First Healer of the East Woods Monastery of Rikitak, Lord of Light... little Cari... I Summon thee, here to your own Wits... By all the strength of my love... I bind thee to answer me now.") Not his True Name; serves.
- ROLL: EL8 (Lv3+Conj2 + Memory of Aubric 2 + Query 1; both Boosts SPENT) -> 0 0 0 1 0 1 0 0 = 2 vs the leash's grip EL4 (Calder's working 3 + demon taint 1) -> 1 0 0 0 = 1. EFFECT 1 - a foothold, narrow.
  - Aubric surfaces - partly, briefly. The filmed eyes clear a little. He knows her. He speaks in fragments (dead throat): "...Cari." / "Don't... look at me, little sister." / (fighting the hunger, he takes a deliberate step BACK from the camp) / "He walks me... to the sea. To the woman... who shamed him." (= MIRA at Saltwind - Calder is steering the ghola to her. Now Caria knows.) Then the leash tugs and he sways; the window is closing - effect 1 buys this exchange and perhaps one more short one before the grip reasserts.
  - Kade HEARS it speak her name. Stops dead. Lowers the chain a hand's breadth. (@@* Kade did not know the "property" was a Brother of the House. He is shaken - and angry.)
- Resources: Tokens 3/3; Hook 1 left this scene; Karma 4 (+1 earmarked). Boosts spent.
- Caria's calm breaks. Hairpin drawn like a poniard; with her aura she SEIZES the leash in the Borderlands (no distance there) while its holder pulls, leans on the silver to bite the cord, pours will and fury down the line - "How fucking DARE you?!" - hoping to burn it out and to send her rage back to the puppeteer. Lee: counts as a REBUKE; she's ready to burn resources. Aubric's foothold (effect 1) offered as an assist.
- GM PRE-ROLL DECISIONS (written before rolling):
  - EL7 = Lv3 + Conj2 + silver hairpin actively wielded 1 + Aubric's assist 1 (he pulls against it from inside).
  - Opposition = the leash's grip EL4 (Calder's working 3 + demon taint 1) - same as the summons.
  - The working has RATING 3. Rebuke effect reduces it; at 0 it SNAPS (burns out end to end). Effect beyond 3 travels up the line and lands on the HOLDER (Calder) as harm (Complication/burn), per the S3 Fennick precedent.
  - On ANY success the holder feels her rage and tastes her flavor (brimstone + Rikitak's fire). Latent: Calder learns who she is -> Purification clock consequences @@.
- ROLL: REBUKE EL7 -> 1 0 1 1 0 0 1 = 4 vs leash EL4 -> 1 1 0 1 = 3. EFFECT 1. Pending Lee's resources:
  - Stands: leash rating 3 -> 2, scorched, holds. Calder feels a jolt and tastes her. Aubric's window stays open a little longer (the pull slackens).
  - +Hook (last 1, flat): effect 2 -> leash 1, holds. Brimstone grips brimstone: Calder tastes the DEMON in her specifically (Purification evidence) -> Hook trigger (Karma/Token).
  - +Token (best result 7): 7 vs 3 -> effect 4 -> leash SNAPS (burns out end to end); 1 excess lands on Calder.
  - +Token +Hook: 8 vs 3 -> effect 5 -> SNAPS; 2 excess on Calder; plus the Hook complication.
- LEE: "FUCK that guy." Spends the HOOK (last 1) and a TOKEN. Considering Karma.
  - Token -> best result 7; +1 Hook flat -> 8 vs 3. EFFECT 5. The leash (rating 3) SNAPS - burns out end to end. 2 EXCESS lands on the holder.
  - Caria Tokens 3 -> 2/3. Hook spent for the scene.
  - HOOK TRIGGER (brimstone grips brimstone; the holder tastes the DEMON in her - Purification evidence) -> +1 Karma (2nd Hook trigger this session; rank 3 allows 3). Karma 4 -> 5 (over cap 4 - must spend down before next session).
  - The holder: HIERARCH VOSS CALDER, at the Monastery. 2 harm as a Complication - the line burns back into his hands: burned palms, a blinding headache, knocked from his chair (@@* recovers in days; humiliated; now KNOWS the flavor of the one who did it: brimstone + Rikitak's fire + a Healer's grief). At the instant before the line burned out, Caria glimpsed the far end: candlelight, a tattooed scalp, a light contralto scream. She KNOWS it's Calder.
  - PURIFICATION LIST (hidden): 2/4 -> 3/4 (direct evidence of demon-taint, tasted by Calder himself). The S2 oath story, when it arrives, still ticks it to 4/4. @@ Consequences to come.
  - Aubric: freed of the leash. Drops to his knees in the reeds. What holds his shade in the body now is only the old binding's residue, the demon taint's hunger, and his own will. He can let go - if he wants to (Asher's point 3). The film on his eyes is thinner.
- Karma note for Lee: RAW, Karma buys nothing more on this roll (a Token already made it the best result; Karma-as-Token is for when a Token can't be spent; Karma "Succeed" is last-ditch). Possible uses: save it, or Enchantment later.
- Caria (too pleased, no regrets) turns to Aubric: "As you honor the life you lived, give me now your True Name in death, that I may truly help you."
- GM (no roll - his choice, his will; the leash is gone and his wits are up): Aubric GIVES his True Name. He crawls to the water's edge, as near as the frost-ring allows without touching it, and says it low - only Caria hears it (off-page; it's the name his mother gave him before the Monastery renamed him, and something older under it. Caria knows it now.)
  - ACCORDS: a True Name given freely is a gift; a gift accepted implies a debt. Aubric names his price, the only price he ever asked anyone: "Feed whoever comes to your back door hungry." (Same words Berra quoted in S2.) Caria now carries that as a debt to him - a way of living, not a single payment. (@@* GM: it counts as paid each time she does it; never fully discharged. Lee's call if he wants it framed otherwise.)
  - Mechanics (@@ UNCLEAR, logged): a freshly given True Name = +2 Boost to any working on him this night (fresh, freely given, not practiced). Making it lasting would be a Maneuver ("True Name of Aubric") bought with Karma at session end - noted as an option.
  - Aubric's plea: "Let me go, little sister. Before I forget myself again." The hunger is winning back ground - effect-5 freedom is not permanent; the demon taint still sits in the body.
- Kade: close enough to hear the plea. Has put the chain away. Standing guard between Aubric and the camp, not to take him - to keep the drovers back.
- Caria (never meant to enslave him; would bind/drive him Down only if the curse took his mind; believes he can CONSENT) reaches across the empowered salt, lays a hand on his scalp: "By the True Name, and the love I bear for thee, I Exorcise thee to thy final rest. May none call thee forth again."
- GM PRE-ROLL DECISIONS (written before rolling):
  - EL9 = Lv3 + Conj2 + True Name (given) +2 + Aubric's consent/assist +1 + silver pin in her fist +1.
  - Opposition EL3 = body-binding residue 2 + demon taint 1 (the leash is gone).
  - Success: Aubric's shade goes on; the body falls. Effect 1-2: the demon taint stays in the corpse (must be burned/salted - Asher can). Effect 3+: the taint is cast out and burns in her salt. "May none call thee forth again" - with effect 3+, it holds as a seal on his rest (@@* no necromancer can raise him again without breaking her working).
  - Failure: the hunger surges; he flinches from her hand; the frost ward (2) meets him if he lunges.
- ROLL: EXORCISM EL9 -> 0 0 0 0 0 1 1 1 1 = 4 vs EL3 -> 1 0 0 = 1. EFFECT 3. FULL SUCCESS.
  - Aubric's shade goes on to his rest. The body falls - only a body now.
  - The demon taint is cast out and BURNS in her salt: the frost ring flares sulphur-yellow along a hand's width where his body lies against it, then goes clean.
  - The SEAL holds: "May none call thee forth again" - no one can raise him again without first breaking her working (@@* rating 3; Calder would have to come through it).
  - His last words (@@* GM): "There. Fire taught you something worth knowing." - and, to her, the old nickname: "Well done, little sister."
- Kade: kneels by the body when it's safe; closes the eyes with two fingers; says the short office for the dead under his breath (he's a Brother too). Then, very quietly, to Caria: "That was a Brother of the House." Latent: Kade now knows what Calder does with the dead; his loyalty to the Almoner's errand is cracked. @@
- Good Fun Karma candidates S4: the exorcism; "How DARE you"; Tessa's kitchen. (Tally at session end.)
- Caria collapses to her knees, crawls to the corpse, holds it, weeps until she passes out. (No roll - exhaustion; no Harm assigned; she's spent.)
  - Her Guidable Fax goes out when she loses consciousness (not Persistent). Both Wards (Persistent) HOLD through the night.
  - Kade does not stop her. When she's out, he unties the Hospitaller's keys (orange ribbon and all) and tucks them into the pocket of Korrin's coat on her. Carries her back to the wagon himself; lays her beside Jem inside the ember ring. Hollis lies across the tailboard.
  - Korrin + Wick wrap the body in sailcloth; it lies by the fire under Asher's watch till morning (he burns it only if she asks). Kade sits up the rest of the night by the body.
  - Nothing else troubles the Ford. Moonset, then dawn.
- NEW GEAR: the Hospitaller's keys (six brass, one iron with orange ribbon - the infirmary poison-cupboard among them). @@ The Monastery will want them back; they open doors in the House.
- Scene 2 (S4): dawn at Tanner's Ford. New scene -> Caria Tokens 2 -> 3/3. Hook budget refreshed (3). GM Tokens 3/3.
- Caria wakes stiff, mud-caked, sore thumb; struggles with Korrin's coat buttons -> the KEYS clink in the pocket; she finds them (Kade's doing).
- Jem is AWAKE, sitting up in the wagon bed wrapped in her yellow robe, with a bowl of Korrin's porridge he's been ordered to finish. Weak but clear-eyed. Still has the oak leaf (warm). Hasn't been told his mother's message yet.
- Korrin (at the tailboard with tea): "Keep the coat, Sister. Give it back in Saltwind." Wagons being yoked; roll within the hour; Saltwind by midday. The sailcloth-wrapped body is on the tail of wagon 3 (Kade's) - he's taking it to Saltwind for burial rather than back north (@@* his choice; he hasn't said so to anyone yet).
- Caria wants ritual and to be CLEAN. Water: Tanner's Ford is a river - plenty, but autumn-cold. Asher can heat a bucket or the kettle. Korrin rigs a sailcloth screen between wagons 1 and 2 without being asked and bawls at the drovers to face the oxen. Caria's spare-clothes duffel (from S1 gear) is in wagon 1: plain linen shift, a spare skirt, stockings. Lye soap: Berra's (S2) - @@ assume she has a sliver. No roll.
- THE BATH (Lee, ritual): she washes away grime and grief; keeps the indignation; works to let go of the rage, the hate, and the PLEASURE she took in Calder's pain ("It was quite delicious, wasn't it?") - prays not to become what she despised. Thanks, in turn: Grolikarnigan (footing), Susheil (water), Rana (cold clarity - justice without relying solely on vengeance), Carterion (craft of a warm bath), Rikitak (the little spark - Asher).
  - GM: the Hook's pull (the demon-trace relishing pain) consciously resisted. Not a trigger (nothing derailed); strong character beat. GOOD FUN Karma candidate (top of the S4 list).
  - Asher, thanked to Rikitak's face: flares, flustered, delighted; says nothing (for once).
- She dresses in the ORANGE robe and sash of a Healer (entitled since S2 advancement; packed in her duffel). The Hospitaller's keys go into the Healer's robe pocket.
- Emerging: Korrin sees orange and says nothing, which for Korrin is a speech. Jem, porridge half gone, stares. Kade, done lashing the body to wagon 3, sees the orange robe and the key-cord at her pocket - and walks over. He wants a word before they roll.
- Caria: "May I brush my hair while we talk, Brother?" (She decides she likes Kade very much.)
- Kade's word (@@* GM, from his established character: pragmatic, keeps hands clean, now shaken and angry):
  - He's taking the body to Saltwind, not north. A grave with the right name on it. He'd like her to say the words over it.
  - His errand was to find the Hierarch's "property." He found it; it's destroyed. That will be his report - true, and nothing more.
  - He knows the refectory rumor: the Hospitaller and the "mushroom tea." He asks nothing about it yet, but he's put it together.
  - He asks whether the Hierarch felt the line burn. (Caria knows: yes, and he tasted her.)
  - Then he offers her the choice: he has a letter to write to the Almoner from Saltwind. "What it says about you is up to you, Sister. I can say you were a pilgrim who kept the camp's light. Or I can say what I saw." (An OFFER, not yet a favor. Accords: if she asks him to shade the truth for her, that's a favor accepted -> a debt to Kade.)
- Caria declines any shading: Kade must tell the absolute truth; she saw "Voss" clearly when she burned the spell out of his hands; she'll tell the truth too, and will tell anyone that Kade conducted himself dutifully and admirably. ACCORDS: no favor requested -> no debt to Kade. Her praise is freely given testimony (a gift he accepts with a nod - he gives his own in return: see below). Square.
- Kade (@@*): a long look at "Voss" - nobody calls the Hierarch by his given name. Then: "The truth will do the Hierarch no favors, Sister. A murdered Brother raised on a leash. The Almoner will want that in writing." (Kade sees the Almoner/Calder fault line; the truth is a weapon pointed at Calder, not her - though Calder's own account of her will be another matter.) And his return testimony: "For my part, I'll write that the Sister laid the Hospitaller to rest with his own name and hers. Whoever reads it can make of that what they like."
- Wagons roll. Caria rides wagon 1 beside Jem. Saltwind by midday.
- Pending: Tessa's message to Jem (not yet delivered). The burial at Saltwind (Caria to say the words). Mira ("Thousand-Voices") - Ivro's letter; Calder was walking the ghola toward her. Bellows's coal to the sea. Korrin's coat.
- LEE (OOC): did she recover her nails? What did the salt cost her?
  - NAILS: they were never hers - Korrin's ~40 ship-nails from the broken keg (S3). Caria returned the tied bag at the Ford (~30). Kade still has his handful (~8) plus the one on Hollis's collar; he'll return them at Saltwind (@@*). Caria carries NO iron now except the cold-iron shavings in her Salt-in-the-Hem. (caria.md S3 line "a few iron nails" corrected.)
  - SALT: cost Caria nothing in coin. It came from Korrin's four fish casks + the cook's sack. GM RULING: it's the caravan's expense - her S2 deal with Korrin is to keep the camp's light and spirit-watch, and warding the camp is that job; like lamp oil, the caravan pays. No Accords debt. Cost to KORRIN: salt is cheap in a town called Saltwind, but the unpacked pike and eel must be re-salted within a day or spoil; one cask is likely a loss (@@* a few silver). Korrin hasn't mentioned it and won't.
  - Her real costs: three drops of blood, a sore thumb, and the bonfire's ash. Her 4 silver is still in drovers' teeth (Korrin collects the teeth-coins at Saltwind - whether Caria's 4 come back to her is @@ open).
- (Lee clarifies: Caria, in character, was wondering about the expense to KORRIN.) Caria asks Korrin on the wagon box.
  - Korrin: salt's cheap in Saltwind; the pike will keep if it's in the packing sheds by evening; she'll likely lose a cask of eel ("eels sulk"). Refuses any talk of payment: "You brought my boy back. Don't you dare talk to me about eels." (Accords: Korrin declines to make it a debt. Settled.)
  - Nails: "Your Brother Kade has eight of my nails and a dog wearing a ninth. I've counted." (She'll have them back.)
  - Teeth-coins: collected at the Saltwind gate; Korrin will return Caria's 4 silver along with everyone else's - "I said I'd have them back or their hides. Yours I'll have back to YOU."
- Caria climbs into the bed, settles Jem's head on her shoulder. To Korrin: "Keep the silver. Who should I ask for a nail?"
  - Korrin (@@*): won't take the silver as payment for eels - takes it "toward the boy's keep" (Jem is hers to feed now). Then digs in her coat and produces a nail: the one she had in her teeth at the reunion - Tam found it in the ruts at dawn. "A nail for four silver. Worst trade I ever made, and don't you tell anyone." ACCORDS: framed as a TRADE (silver for a nail) -> balanced, no debt; the surplus is a gift to Jem, not to Korrin.
  - NEW GEAR: one cold-wrought ship-nail, Korrin's (bitten). Iron: Protected 1 vs fey when carried (per ruling); more if worked into a Ward.
  - Caria's purse: ~20 copper, no silver.
- Caria sees the sea (she saw it once as a child from a hilltop tree, a shining haze under the Arch). "I'm going to put my feet in it, and give it a coal of Old Bellows." Feeds the coal, with apologies.
- ROLL (GM honesty check - coal unfed ~20h since the woods): 2d EL2 -> 1 0 = 1. LOW but alive. Asher (riding the fire-pot since dawn) admits he found it down to a sullen red eye when they rolled out and has been breathing on it all morning. With fresh charcoal it catches, grumbles, and comes up hot. Bellows's coal is SULKING (@@* flavor: it smells faintly of hot iron and offended dignity). All is well - near thing.
- Caria too tired and sore to run ahead. Asks Asher to explain to Bellows why - not to excuse, only so he understands; soon.
- Asher relays (the whole night: the Court, the fuelless hearth, the ghola, the Brother laid to rest). Bellows's coal (@@*): goes quiet, then a slow deep glow. Via Asher: "A fire that burns on NOTHING? In a fey house?" - professional interest outweighs the sulk. Then, grudging: "Tell the girl a forge doesn't die of one cold night. Tell her to mind her own coals." Mollified. (Latent: Bellows now wants to hear about that hearth - a future conversation.)
- LEE (OOC): what did Caria get of the Saltwind story at the Monastery? Answer from ledger (her KNOWLEDGE only):
  - Ivro (S1): Calder went to the coast "to recover the Almoner's property," came back empty-handed and furious. Ivro had heard the gossip and was delighted; hence the letter to "Thousand-Voices."
  - Kitchen gossip (garbled): a village shrine-keeper "witch" and a mob of fishwives drove off a priest of Rikitak.
  - Merrit's register: Saltwind's Susheil shrine kept by blind elder Karinna; "an unordained hedge shrine-keeper also serves" - Mira.
  - Olwin (Calder's coachman, bloodied cudgel, drinking since the coast; Calder blamed him for the "riot") - she never spoke to him.
  - NEW (S4): Aubric - "He walks me to the sea, to the woman who shamed him" = Mira.
  - She does NOT know (hidden): Saltwind has been EXPECTING a white-haired woman from the Monastery who sent a fire-demon to scout "what burns" (Asher's S1 scouting was noticed by Shimmer/Min/Mira). She arrives into suspicion.
- Mourner's band: little black cloth. Caria tears a strip from the hem of her ruined shift and rubs it dark with forge charcoal - a smudged grey-black band, tied on her left arm over the orange. (@@ Averond mourning custom unconfirmed - black assumed per Lee.)
- Caria helps Jem make his own band (linen, charcoal), ties it on his arm, meets his eyes, returns the leaf to his hand. Says nothing.
- At the gate she leaves the watchman to his "due and rightful prejudice" - earn respect or suffer vitriol; a mourner may be humble. (No roll.)
- GATE (@@* GM): old watchman (@@* Harl Pennock) and young one (@@* Davey). Korrin handles the gate as caravan master; known here. Harl asks Korrin about "the Sister with the white lock and the fire-thing." Korrin, flat: "She's with me. She brought a boy out of the Weeping Court last night and laid a dead Brother to rest at the Ford. You'll mind your manners." Harl's eyes go to the two mourning bands, the boy, the sailcloth on wagon 3 - he doesn't soften, but he doesn't spit either. He sends Davey running up into the town (to Karinna/Mira - word that the Monastery woman with the fire-demon has arrived). Caravan passes the gate.
- Caria realizes Saltwind expected her: Asher's S1 scouting was noticed (the undine doused him). Thanks Korrin. Tells Asher to keep low.
- Asher: "I didn't TELL her anything. She put me out in a horse-trough." Burrows down into the fire-pot with Bellows's coal (mutual grumbling) - no visible spark.
- Korrin: a grunt that means "you're welcome." Caravan winds down to Korrin's usual yard by the packing sheds at the harbor's east end. Kade's wagon 3 peels off toward the churchyard lane above the harbor (the body). Fennick climbs down with his lap-desk, looks at the town, looks at Caria, writes nothing.
- Lee (OOC): apologized for short turns (grieving, recuperating, preparing). GM: no apology needed.
- Caria to Asher: not scolding, only trying to understand. (Asher, from the pot: a small relieved warmth.)
- Caria to Fennick: sorry, she meant to see he was well; "It has been..." (shrug).
- Fennick (@@* GM): well. Slept at the Ford with Ada's whistle in his fist and the silver in his cheek; no dreams. He SAW the thing in the reeds by her flame, heard her shout. He knew the Hospitaller by sight - and he is the clerk who ENTERED the Hospitaller's death in the House register as "misadventure: ill-chosen mushrooms," on the Almoner's office's instruction, without question. "I will be correcting an entry, Sister." (Latent: Fennick's register amendment = documentary evidence against Calder; dangerous for Fennick.) He looks at her orange robe and mourning band and says: "It suits you. The orange." Then goes to find the harbor-master's office.
- Caria ("Small sparks will build a roaring hearth"): checks Jem - he wants nothing but sleep and asks if she'll be near; Korrin takes charge of him (a cot in the yard office by the stove). Tessa's message held until he's stronger.
- She climbs down and orients. Visible from Korrin's yard (@@* GM geography, consistent with S1 chart - pier, west cove, Gallows Teeth):
  - RANA'S SHRINE: yes - a small grey stone cairn-shrine on the WEST bluff above the town, wind-scoured, a path zigzagging up to it; a thread of pale smoke or mist at its top.
  - SUSHEIL'S SHRINE: down on the waterfront by the pier's root - a low well-house with a blue-painted lintel, steps down to the water (Karinna's).
  - The squat chapel above the harbor where Kade went (Lord of Light / general House chapel @@).
  - Offshore west, beyond the cove: black rocks with white water - the Gallows Teeth.
  - Up the harbor street, outside a house by the well-house, a knot of people gathering - Davey among them, pointing down toward Korrin's yard.
- GM CONSISTENCY NOTE: per S1 (line ~131), Asher DID let slip to Shimmer (lovesick) that "the one with the white in her hair" from "the mountain house" sent him and was coming with the wagons. So his S4 line "I didn't TELL her anything" was a FIB - embarrassed, lovesick shading of the truth to Caria. Caria's intuition was right. Latent: if she presses, he'll confess (sheepish). Not a breach of any Accord (no promise of full report was made? @@ check - the scouting bargain was for mood/burnables/anchor; the slip wasn't part of the report).
- Caria: "I'll be about" (to Jem, into the wind). Asks: where would Mira's home be?
  - Caria's knowledge: NO address. Merrit's register: "an unordained hedge shrine-keeper also serves" (no location). Ivro's letter is addressed only to "Thousand-Voices," into her hand only. Asher's S1 scouting named: warehouse (nets/rope), thatched huts around a LODGE, the lodge hall (timber and stone), Karinna's hut/Susheil well-house. Mira not placed.
  - Inference available: ask at Susheil's well-house (Karinna); the crowd gathering by the well-house is probably where Saltwind's people will come from.
  - OOC to Lee: Mira (and Min, Shimmer, the Warden, Hobbe) are from his earlier Saltwind playtest - asked whether he has canon for Mira's home before GM places it.
- LEE CANON: MIRA'S HOME - west, between the Gallows Teeth and the village; a short walk OUTSIDE the village proper; not easily seen.
- Caria eats Mags's rations on the wagon's running board: hard cheese, the last hard loaf, dried apples; washes it down with well-water from Korrin's yard (and a swallow of brandy). Keeps the honey jar ("for the shrine-keeper") untouched.
- GM ORACLE (2d): crowd's temper -> 1 0 = 1: WARY. They watch from the top of the harbor street and don't come down. Davey's account carried both halves (the fire-demon woman, AND the boy from the Weeping Court + the dead Brother), so the town is divided. After a while ONE comes down (@@* GM): KARINNA, the blind elder of Susheil's well-house, stick in hand, led by a girl of about ten (@@* Nell). The crowd lets her. She stops a few paces off and turns her face toward Caria. First words pending.
- Caria finishes, tidies herself, sets aside her sword (parazonium) and staff on the wagon, walks to Karinna, sets down her satchel, KNEELS and prostrates - face to the paving between flat empty hands. Waits.
- Karinna (@@* GM voice: old coast woman, dry, fair, unafraid): Nell tells her in a whisper what the Sister is doing. Karinna: "Hm." Long pause, listening. She hears/feels: two fires in the pot on the wagon (Asher + Bellows's coal), the salt in the girl's hem, and UNDER it all something older and hotter in the girl herself - the brimstone. First words: "You're the one who sent fire up my street to count what burns." Then: "And there's more than fire on you, child. There's something under it that smells like a struck match." 
  - HOOK TRIGGER offered (vetoable by Lee): her mark draws exactly the wrong notice at the worst moment (a humble approach to a suspicious shrine-keeper) -> would be the 3rd trigger this session (rank 3 allows 3 Karma) -> +1 Karma.
- Karinna does not tell her to rise. Yet.
- Lee TAKES the Hook trigger (3rd this session, rank 3 -> pays). Karma 5 -> 6 (+1 earmarked Conjuror). Over cap 4: must spend down before next session.
- Caria, unhidden: "Yes, honored Keeper. He knew I meant no harm in it. I knew he would do none. I will do penance for the worry it might have caused you and yours, as you see fit."
- Karinna (@@* GM): "Get up off my cobbles. I'm too old to watch young folk kneel in fish-scale." Nell helps Caria rise. Then she names the penance - a test as much as a penance: come down to the well-house and put both hands into Susheil's water, "and let her tell me what you are. Water doesn't lie to me." (Resonance: Caria prayed to Susheil this very morning for cleansing.)
  - ACCORDS: penance OFFERED by Caria, NAMED by Karinna - when Caria performs it, the worry-debt is paid and concluded.
  - MECHANICS pending (@@ GM): the water's reading - a Susheil anchor reads her truly; no roll to "pass" if she's honest - but the brimstone will show. What Susheil shows Karinna: decide at the well (lean: a girl of fire and salt with a struck match in her, who has been weeping into rivers - and who bled into salt last night for other people).
- Caria takes satchel and fire-pot, walks down to the well-house past the watching street; pays the due without complaint. Fire-pot left at the door (Asher and Bellows inside, both uneasy this near the wet anchor).
- Inside: a low stone room, steps down to a basin fed by the sea through a channel, blue-painted rim, shells and small offerings. Hands in to the wrist.
- ROLL Karinna's reading through Susheil: EL4 (@@* Lv3 + anchor 1) -> 0 0 1 1 = 2 vs Diff 1 -> 0. EFFECT 2 - clear on the recent and the essential; NOT deep ancestry (the Contract stays hidden; would need 3+).
  - Physical: the water hisses faintly and a thread of steam rises off her wrists; the water around her hands warms - then settles, and holds her.
  - Karinna sees: the struck-match mark is BORN, not chosen or bargained for; the girl fights it (this morning's prayer); salt and blood given for others last night; a boy brought up out of the fey dark; a dead Brother laid down gently by his own name; grief like a flood; and a line burned end to end with fury - and at the far end of it, a man she KNOWS: "the priest who came here with his cudgel-man." She felt Calder's taste in Saltwind two weeks ago.
  - Verdict (@@*): "You're no spy of that mountain. You're something it made and couldn't keep." She keeps a hand over Caria's in the water a moment. Penance done -> worry-debt concluded (Accords).
  - Then: "Thousand-Voices will want to see you. And she'll want to know what you did to that priest's leash, because it was her door he was walking it to."
- Caria: "...done? Oh, no, honored Keeper. My order has done WRONGS here, and I will not have it." Then: "Apologies. I meant no disrespect."
- Karinna (@@*): "Then stay and do more. That's not penance, girl. Round here we call that being neighbors." Sends Nell to fetch Thousand-Voices. (Mira's meeting opens S5.)

## END OF S04 (Lee called the wrap, 2026-09-30 ~00:35) - Susheil's well-house, Saltwind, midday; Mira sent for
- Karma: 2 carried + 1 attendance + 3 Hook triggers (like-calls-like Sight; the leash; Karinna's nose) = 6. GM Good Fun +1 (the bath at the Ford: gratitude to five gods and the prayer "let me not become what I have despised" - with the exorcism as its twin) -> 7 hard. Plus 1 earmarked Conjuror (banked S3). No Caria extremes in S4.
- Cap at Level 3 = 4. Must spend down to 4 before S5. Level 4 costs 4 (needs Rested + healed: rest in Saltwind). Conjuror 2->3 costs 2 (earmark 1 + 1 hard). One improvement per session (earmark-only advances excepted, per S01 ruling). Spend pending (Lee).
- Tokens end 3/3. Harm: none. Wards at the Ford: left behind (camp struck). Hook budget n/a.
- Lee proposes TWO upgrades (S4 = two sessions' worth). GM agrees: split at dawn at the Ford; no extra attendance Karma. Pending Lee's picks. Natural pair: Level 4 (4) + Conjuror 3 (earmark 1 + 1 hard) = 5 hard -> 2 left (cap 5 at Level 4).
- LEE S4 SPEND: Level 3 -> 4 (4K); Spellweaver 2 -> 3 (2K) with the new Maneuver rank in ARTIFICE 1 (@@ scope). Third free known spell: Lee choosing. Karma 7 -> 1 carried (+1 earmarked Conjuror).
- FILES DELIVERED (2026-09-30): S04 summary, sheet, gap log, ledger.
- LEE: third free known spell = TANGLEBONES (Grasping Ghosts / Entangle). Design to be worked out in-fiction in downtime.
  - GM notes offered to Lee (@@*, suggestions only): (1) ethics seam - if it borrows the local dead's grasp, it brushes against Calder's sin (using the dead without leave); if it wakes the TARGET's own past to hold them ("individual pasts that claw and drag at us"), it stays clean and fits her; (2) systemic hook - the caster could tap the target's own Hook against them as dice (the S4 exploiter rule); (3) likely Hindrance-shaped (effect = hold), stronger where the dead are thick or the target's past is heavy.
- LEE: Tanglebones is CANON (spells.md). Lilita missed it - riffed on design before checking. Sheet corrected to the canon text. Gap log: corrections logged (Tanglebones; Improvised Traits already covered by shaping-effects.md; the Accords already have a section in conjuring.md).
- PROCEDURE CHANGE from S5 (Lee): Improvised/Traited spells use the CANON TWO-STEP - (1) free-action HOLD roll vs raised Difficulty (fail = spell falls apart, destabilized); (2) normal CAST roll at full power. Past casts stand as rolled (no retcon). GM to note how it feels in play for a later comparison write-up.
- LEE CANON: Accords bind minds only; spells are mechanistic and outside them; rarely a spell spawns a new-minted spirit that may persist. GM NOTE: this fits the S3 hidden latent exactly - the Court's Enchanted hearth-fire (Caria's, permanent, on the house's current, with "something of her in it") may one day wake a salamander. That spirit would be born of HER spell - and born under the Accords. Keep hidden; let it grow.

## SESSION 5 - started 2026-09-30 09:10 (Lee's clock) - Saltwind, Susheil's well-house, midday
- Caria Level 4: Luck Tokens 4/4. +1 attendance Karma -> 2 carried (+1 earmarked Conjuror); cap 5. Hook A Hint of Brimstone 3 (budget 3/scene; pays Karma up to 3 triggers/session). GM Tokens 3/3. Harm none (tired; a night's rest not yet taken - @@ she hasn't slept properly since the wagon; Level-up "Rested" requirement was waived as downtime-in-the-gap, per wrap).
- PROCEDURE: Improvised/Traited spells now two-step (hold, then cast).
- Caria at the basin: shame for all the unthanked scrubbing; "Am I doomed to tears again?" - tears are water too, making her clean again. Outside in the cold, hands raised to the sea, weeping and laughing with joy.
- MIRA ("Thousand-Voices") arrives (@@* GM voice drawn from canon vignette in action/conjuror.md: gentle, patient, empathic medium; lets people come to the telling in their own time; lends her own body to spirits). Late 20s @@, sea-weathered, bare feet, shawl, hair salt-stiff; Nell at her side. SHIMMER (undine) rides her: a ripple of light in a waterskin/at her wrist; Shimmer remembers the horse-trough.
  - Mira's Third Eye (no roll - practiced, open subject): grief and joy tangled; the struck-match mark; salt; two fires waiting in a pot by the door (one of which Shimmer has doused before); and the faint warm anchor-line running off inland (the Court's hearth).
  - She does not interrupt. She waits on the sea-wall until the laughter runs down.
- Caria shows her open, empty hands to the waterskin (Lee recalls a message Shimmer sent back with Asher about this - NO RECORD in the ledger of any message; S1 has only the dousing. OOC note to Lee to supply it if he remembers one; the gesture reads fine regardless: "no fire in my hands").
  - Shimmer: a slow, considering ripple; the waterskin's leather cools against Mira's hip. Grudging acceptance (@@*): not friendship, a truce.
- Caria: "Mira Thousand-Voices of Saltwind? Itinerant shrine-keeper of the coast?" - warm greeting.
- Mira (@@*): "Thousand-Voices is what they call me when they're being polite. Mira will do." Notes the charcoal mourning band; she felt the fresh loss before she saw it. "You're in mourning, and you've come to my coast with your hands open. Sit with me a moment, before the whole street decides what to think of you." Pats the wall beside her.
- LEE CANON (S1, secondhand via Asher): Shimmer's message was to come showing open hands - she expected oil and a torch. Caria has now fulfilled it. (Shimmer's truce stands on that.)
- Caria prostrates to Mira as to Karinna - knees, face, hands flat to the stones - and waits.
- Mira (@@*, per her canon manner - "settling beside him in the sand"): does not stand over her. Gets down off the wall and sits on the cobbles facing her, same level, knees nearly touching; lays one hand flat on the stones beside Caria's. "I'm here. Take your time." Waits. (Nell, scandalized, stays on the wall. Shimmer ripples.)
- Caria's formal address to Mira: the Order's wayward hands wronged Saltwind, Mira most of all; "I have personally burned those hands, and I am not done..." (checks herself - not why she's here); restitution; she prays to be the instrument of that justice, but she is HERE; she'll strive by all means - penance, restitution, earnest aid - to Mira, the village, its shrines; she'll cook, clean pots, mend nets; "I am not the great healer master Aub-" - can't say it. Tries again. Can't yet.
- Mira (@@*): gives her the room. Lays her hand over Caria's on the stones now (touch). Says only: "I'm not going anywhere." Mira's Third Eye catches the grief pinned on a name, and the cold weight in the Healer's pocket (the keys) - she doesn't press. Shimmer: the waterskin goes warm (!) against Mira's hip - an undine's version of sympathy.
- Caria clasps Mira's hand; "Aubric was my FRIEND." She'll avenge him on her own time; may never be the Healer/teacher he was; "How can I help?"
- Mira (@@*): 
  - First: sleep and food - "you look like the tide dragged you here." Offers her own hearth (west of the village, past the cove) OR Karinna's loft - hospitality OFFERED (Accords: guest-right if accepted).
  - Then: the burial - she saw the lean Brother and his grey dog go up to the churchyard with a sailcloth bundle. She'll come. "The village should see you there. A mountain girl mourning a mountain Brother the mountain killed. That will say more than any penance."
  - Then, work: Karinna's hands aren't what they were; the boats come in with torn nets and torn hands; winter cough starting in the huts by the lodge; hungry folk this autumn - "a Healer who'll work for bread would be a gift." (Resonates with Aubric's price: feed whoever comes to your back door hungry.)
  - And later, rested: tell her what she did to the priest's leash - "because it was my door he was walking it to."
- Caria ACCEPTS Mira's hearth (guest-right, Accords: an Agreement - neither may directly harm the other; guests extend to her company). "I have something for you yet" (Ivro's letter - not yet given). Fetches the fire-pot: "May my friends come?"
- Mira (@@*): looks into the pot with the Third Eye; consults Shimmer (waterskin sloshes). Verdict: YES - "a hearth is for fires." Terms relayed from Shimmer: the little one keeps to the hearth and stops asking what burns; the old one is welcome because "he's too cross to be any trouble." Mira also sees what the old coal WANTS (the sea) - she smiles: "And that one's come a long way to meet my water, hasn't he." (Hint: the coal's delivery can happen at Mira's cove.) Asher (in the pot): mortified, agrees to everything. Bellows: a grudging warm pulse.
- The walk west (no roll): Caria half-closes her eyes and follows by sound - Mira's bare feet, Nell's chatter (sent home at the last houses), gulls, surf. Cliff path past the west cove; the Gallows Teeth white-fringed offshore.
- MIRA'S HOME (Lee canon: west, between the Teeth and the village, short walk out, not easily seen). GM detail (@@*, light touch - Lee may overwrite): a low stone cottage tucked into a fold of the cliff below the path's line of sight, turf roof, driftwood door, net-floats and shells strung by the door, smoke from one chimney, a rain-barrel (Shimmer's). Arrival at the threshold; Mira holds the door.
- Caria remembers "Nell." Sees the cottage: "It's lovely." Sets the fire-pot on Mira's hearthstone, kneels, understands the House's longing for a hearth, smiles (she did a good thing). To Asher: visit the House with her love; she's lonely. Falls asleep in a heap on the hearthstone.
- Asher: goes by the anchor line (Caria's permanent thread to the Court's hearth) - fire to fire, no roll (a known road, an anchored destination). Bellows's coal settles into the edge of Mira's peat fire, grumbling contentedly.
- Mira: pulls her boots off, drags the quilt over her, tucks a folded shawl under her head; doesn't move her from the hearthstone. Shimmer's rain-barrel ripples once at the door.
- Caria sleeps through the afternoon (Rested). Wakes near dusk: fish-and-barley stew on the hook, Mira mending a net at the table, Asher back on the hearth-log bright with news: the House sang when he gave her Caria's love; she sends back "a warm leaf" (@@* - a sense of warmth along the anchor line, nothing physical).
- Good Fun Karma candidates S5: the tears-are-water laughter at the shrine; "Aubric was my friend."
- Caria rises (stiff, wonderfully rested), hungry, but duty first: draws Ivro's letter from her purse and offers it to Mira in silence. (Errand from S1: into Thousand-Voices' hand only - DELIVERED.)
- Mira takes it in both hands. Third Eye on the seal: Ivro's salamander, with a watching spark in the wax. The spark regards Mira, then Caria - and winks out, content (the ward's condition met: the right hand). Asher, on the hearth-log, recognizes a cousin-spark of the Precentor's and sits up very straight.
- Mira reads (contents per S1 ledger, GM-only until shared): Ivro introduces himself as a friend of the old faith; asks what Calder was hunting on her coast; offers the shelter of his network; warns the Almoner's collectors ride the caravans; asks her to judge Caria for herself.
- Mira shares only the last: "He asks me to judge you for myself." Folds it. "I have." Then, nodding at the pot: "Now eat, before you fall over again. We'll talk about your Precentor and his friends over the bowl." (She'll discuss the rest at table.)
- Accords/errand: Ivro's letter-errand COMPLETE.
- Caria eats and ENJOYS it - her first real hot meal since Berra's kitchen at Crossroads, two nights and a lifetime ago. Mira refills without asking. Asher and Bellows share the peat fire in something like peace.
- After the second bowl, Mira opens the talk (@@*): Ivro asks what the priest was hunting on her coast. "I can tell you what he SAID he was hunting. I think you can tell me what it was." (She suspects; Caria knows - Aubric, the ghola, walked toward her door.)
- Caria: "WILL you tell me what he said? I will not lie to you, but I owe him courtesy and many debts, and would rather not contradict him to the secondhand ears of his superiors." (Ambiguous "him" - read as: she wants the official story first so her truth is placed carefully.)
- Mira (@@* - consistent with ledger canon: "talk of property," cudgel-man, fishwives; Lee's earlier Saltwind playtest may have fixed the exact words - OOC flag to Lee): Calder said the Almoner's PROPERTY had strayed - "a servant of the House, sick in his mind, who wandered from his keepers" - dangerous, to be returned, not harmed. He demanded the huts searched, the caves under the Teeth, the well-house, her house; anyone sheltering it would answer to the Mountain. Mira's observation: "He called it 'it' one breath and 'him' the next." Then Olwin laid a cudgel on the Pellow boy (@@*) who wouldn't move off a doorstep, and the fishwives came down the hill with gutting knives, and the priest left. (Keep details light; Lee to correct.)
